# AVRL research ledger

Entries are appended by the autoresearch loop and by hand. Verdicts are against the pre-registered thresholds in PROJECT.md and EXPERIMENT_BACKLOG.md.

## E01 2026-09-30 kill (for claim 3 on this task/proxy pair)
Hypothesis: proxy error (group majority answer wrong) has exploitable structure in prompt space and is not already captured by cheap features.
Run: Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct, GSM8K train, 3,000 prompts x 8 rollouts (vLLM, temperature 1.0), proxy = agreement with group majority, verifier = gold answer. Files: runs/p0/summary.json, result.json, feats.parquet, sim_*.json, analyze.log.

| quantity | value |
|---|---|
| rollout accuracy / majority accuracy | 64.2% / 85.5% |
| group proxy-error rate (majority wrong) | 14.5% |
| proxy error, unanimous groups (18.6% of groups) | 0.5% |
| proxy error, split groups | 17.7% |
| AUC for proxy error: agreement only / all cheap / embedding only / cheap + embedding | 0.914 / 0.923 / 0.606 / 0.922 |
| neighbor ratio of proxy error by embedding cell (k = 30 / 100 / 300) | 1.00 / 1.03 / 1.04 |
| confident subset (agreement >= 0.75, n = 1,551): error rate / cheap AUC / neighbor ratio | 1.1% / 0.63 / 1.16 |

Allocation replay (errors caught per budget, 100 cells, 8 seeds; columns: uniform, static with free labels [approximately the agreement threshold], static budget-matched, TS flat, greedy online self-trained, TS online, TS + free prior, neighbor, oracle):
10%: 28, 165, 98, 31, 138, 95, 118, 53, 218. 25%: 76, 264, 212, 83, 253, 208, 232, 117, 309. 50%: 157, 299, 287, 164, 296, 288, 294, 206, 309. Embedding features change nothing.

Verdict: pre-registered read in PROJECT.md triggers (embeddings add < 0.05 AUC, neighbor ratio < 1.5): claim 3 (edge from prompt-space structure) is dead on GSM8K with a majority-vote proxy. The fixed agreement threshold is near-optimal here; the self-training allocator matches it at 25% and 50% budgets and loses at 10%. This is the "threshold regime" and becomes the first point of the structure-statistic-versus-gain curve (see the revised proposal): cheap-feature AUC 0.92, gain of learned over threshold about 0.
What it changes: the allocator can only earn its keep where the proxy lacks a confidence signal. Next: E04 (small judge model as proxy, no self-consistency), E05 (self-verification proxy), E08 (code task where majority vote over programs is ill-defined), each reporting the same three statistics plus the replay, to populate the curve. Also add an explicit label-free agreement-threshold policy to the replay so the baseline is not approximated by "static with free labels".

## G01 2026-10-18 kill (label-free baselines on the E01 replay, cheap features, GSM8K majority-vote proxy)
Hypothesis: the self-trained greedy online allocator (learned, no gold at selection time) catches at least 5 pp more proxy errors per verifier call than a label-free recalibrated agreement threshold (recal_threshold) at the 10% or 25% budget, with the gap above 2x the larger seed sd. Null: it does not.
Run: avrl/exp/g01_label_free_baselines.py, CPU only, no GPU job. Sim split rebuilt as runs/sim_generic.py: 2,180 groups, 75 cell100 buckets, 309 proxy errors. Seeds 0 to 7. Outputs: avrl/runs/G01/result.json, table.md, run.log, done.flag. Diagnostic (not part of the verdict): avrl/exp/g01_diag_threshold_cold.py, avrl/runs/G01/diag_threshold_cold/.
Reproduction gate (K = 1, the K of the E01 reference): greedy_online and static_matched match avrl/runs/p0/sim_cell100_cheap.json at 10, 25, 50% with diff 0.000 hits. Passed.

Hit rate (%) = proxy errors caught / verifier calls, mean over seeds, sd over seeds in pp (K = 1):

| budget | calls | uniform | fixed agreement threshold | recal threshold | greedy online (learned) | static matched | oracle | static, free labels (ref only) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 5% | 109 | 12.0 ± 3.4 | 83.1 ± 2.1 | 85.6 ± 2.3 | 60.0 ± 3.8 | 49.3 ± 4.0 | 100.0 | 85.3 |
| 10% | 218 | 12.8 ± 2.3 | 72.0 ± 0.8 | 65.8 ± 2.5 | 63.2 ± 0.9 | 44.8 ± 1.9 | 100.0 | 75.7 |
| 25% | 545 | 13.9 ± 1.3 | 46.3 ± 0.3 | 45.5 ± 0.7 | 46.4 ± 0.3 | 38.9 ± 1.0 | 56.7 | 48.4 |
| 50% | 1090 | 14.4 ± 0.6 | 27.3 ± 0.0 | 27.2 ± 0.5 | 27.2 ± 0.0 | 26.3 ± 0.3 | 28.3 | 27.4 |

Primary contrast, greedy online minus recal threshold (pp of hit rate): 5%: -25.6, 10%: -2.6, 25%: +0.9, 50%: -0.0. Secondary, greedy minus fixed threshold: 5%: -23.2, 10%: -8.8, 25%: +0.0, 50%: -0.1.
Pass rule (>= 5 pp and > 2x max seed sd at 10% or 25%): not met (10%: -2.6, 25%: +0.9). Kill rule (within 3 pp or below at all four budgets): met.

Verdict: KILL. In this regime the label-free threshold family is the deliverable, and the learned allocator's edge over the baselines is not supported. E01's edge over static_matched is reproduced by a label-free rule (recal 143.5 vs static_matched 97.6 hits at 10%), which supports the null: that edge comes from static_matched spending half its budget on random exploration, not from the allocator.

Notes:
- K: the spec says K = 4, but the E01 reference JSON was written by avrl/runs/p0/sim_alloc.py (K = 1). Both were run. Per-seed hits are identical for greedy and recal at K = 1 and K = 4, because the refit points (40, 40, 68, 136 labels) are multiples of 4. The verdict does not depend on K.
- Cold start: the replay greedy ranks randomly until its first refit (37% of the 5% budget, 18% at 10%, 12% at 25% and 50%). allocators.GreedyOnlineAllocator uses the threshold order there instead. Diagnostic with a threshold cold start: greedy 62.4 / 62.3 / 45.9 / 27.6 at 5 / 10 / 25 / 50%, gap to recal -23.2 / -3.6 / +0.4 / +0.4 pp. The kill still holds, so the cold start is not the cause.
- The label-free recal is not uniformly the best label-free rule. At 10% the plain fixed threshold (72.0%) beats recal (65.8%) and greedy (63.2%). Recal wins at 5% (+2.5 pp over fixed).
- The seed sd comes from tie-break draws only (the split is fixed), so it understates split-level uncertainty. Single split, cheap features only.
- Interpretation limit: this is a replay on E01's sim split with cheap features. A pass or kill here is necessary, not sufficient, for claims 1 and 2. Policy quality under GRPO is untested (avrl/p1_grpo.py absent).
- E04 to E06 were gated on G01 being inconclusive or passing. G01 killed, so no GPU job was launched.

## G01 2026-10-18 kill
Hypothesis: on E01's GSM8K majority-vote features, the self-trained greedy online allocator (learned arm, no gold labels at selection time) catches at least 5 pp more proxy errors per verifier call than a label-free recalibrated agreement threshold (recal_threshold) at the 10% or 25% budget, with the gap above 2x the larger seed sd. Null: it does not.

Run: avrl/exp/g01_label_free_baselines.py, CPU only, no GPU job. No new generation: the replay reuses E01's rollouts (Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct, GSM8K train, 3,000 prompts x 8 rollouts, proxy = agreement with group majority, verifier = gold answer). Sim split rebuilt as runs/sim_generic.py: n = 2,180 groups in 75 cell100 buckets, 309 proxy errors (base rate 14.2%). Budgets 5, 10, 25, 50% of n (109, 218, 545, 1,090 verifier calls). Seeds 0 to 7 (tie-break draws only, split fixed). Cheap features only: agreement, n_distinct, mean_tokens, std_tokens, any_truncated, log_qlen. Reproduction gate at K = 1: greedy_online and static_matched match avrl/runs/p0/sim_cell100_cheap.json at 10, 25, 50% with zero diff. Outputs: avrl/runs/G01/ (result.json, table.md, run.log, done.flag). This entry supersedes the automatic G01 block above, which has the same numbers; they were rechecked against result.json and table.md, and fixed_agreement_threshold and recal_threshold were recomputed independently per seed (identical hits).

Hit rate (%) = proxy errors caught / verifier calls, mean over seeds, sd over seeds in pp (K = 1; K = 4 gives identical numbers):

| budget (calls) | uniform | fixed agreement threshold | recal threshold | greedy online (learned) | static matched | oracle | static, free labels (ref only) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 5% (109) | 12.0 ± 3.4 | 83.1 ± 2.1 | 85.6 ± 2.3 | 60.0 ± 3.8 | 49.3 ± 4.0 | 100.0 | 85.3 |
| 10% (218) | 12.8 ± 2.3 | 72.0 ± 0.8 | 65.8 ± 2.5 | 63.2 ± 0.9 | 44.8 ± 1.9 | 100.0 | 75.7 |
| 25% (545) | 13.9 ± 1.3 | 46.3 ± 0.3 | 45.5 ± 0.7 | 46.4 ± 0.3 | 38.9 ± 1.0 | 56.7 | 48.4 |
| 50% (1,090) | 14.4 ± 0.6 | 27.3 ± 0.0 | 27.2 ± 0.5 | 27.2 ± 0.0 | 26.3 ± 0.3 | 28.3 | 27.4 |

Raw hits at 10%: fixed 156.9, recal 143.5, greedy 137.8, static_matched 97.6, static 165.0, oracle 218.

Primary contrast, greedy minus recal (pp of hit rate): 5%: -25.6 (2x max seed sd 7.6), 10%: -2.6 (5.0), 25%: +0.9 (1.3), 50%: -0.0 (1.0). Secondary, greedy minus fixed: -23.2, -8.8, +0.0, -0.1.

Verdict: KILL, by the pre-registered rules. Pass not met: no budget reaches +5 pp at 10% or 25% (-2.6 and +0.9). Kill met: at all four budgets greedy is within 3 pp of recal or below it. No gap falls in the 3 to 5 pp inconclusive band.

What it changes:
- Claim 2 (learned allocator admits fewer corrupted rewards than any fixed rule at equal budget): not supported on this replay. The learned arm is below the plain fixed threshold at 5% and 10%, below recal at 5%, and ties both at 25% and 50%. E01's edge over static_matched (138 vs 98 hits at 10%) is reproduced by a label-free rule (recal, 143.5 hits), so that edge comes from static_matched spending half its budget on random exploration, not from the allocator.
- Claim 3 (the allocator's edge comes from prompt-space structure): not supported for this task/proxy pair. Once a label-free baseline is used there is no allocator edge left to attribute to structure, and E01 found embedding features add nothing (AUC cheap 0.923, cheap plus embedding 0.922). Scope: GSM8K with majority-vote proxy only.
- The label-free recal threshold is the comparator to beat in this regime. Thesis claims should be tested against it, not against static_matched or the free-label static classifier.
- Caveats: replay only, with no GRPO training (avrl/p1_grpo.py absent), so policy-quality claim 1 is untested. Cheap features only. One split; the seed sd covers tie-breaks only, so split-level uncertainty is not measured. The kill is not a cold-start artifact: the diagnostic with a threshold cold start (avrl/exp/g01_diag_threshold_cold.py, avrl/runs/G01/diag_threshold_cold/) gives greedy 62.4 / 62.3 / 45.9 / 27.6 at 5 / 10 / 25 / 50%, gaps to recal -23.2 / -3.6 / +0.4 / +0.4 pp.
- At 10% the plain fixed threshold (72.0) beats recal (65.8). Claim 2 is stated against any fixed rule, so the bar is the best fixed rule, not recal.
- E04 to E06 stay unlaunched: their gate was G01 inconclusive or pass, and G01 killed.

Follow-ups (CPU only; ids in lit/avrl/EXPERIMENT_BACKLOG.md under "Proposed by the loop"):
1. L1a, split robustness (about 10 min). Repeat G01 over 8 fit/sim splits. Expected information value high: it tests whether the kill and the 10% gap of -2.6 pp survive split-level noise, which the current seed sd cannot measure.
2. L1b, strongest label-free rule (about 15 min). Sweep prior strength, refit cadence and stratum grid on split seeds 0 to 3, report on 4 to 7. Expected information value high for claim 2: it fixes the real bar, since recal is not the best fixed rule at 10%.
3. L1c, learned-arm ceiling (about 15 min). Swap the logistic scorer for gradient-boosted trees on the same cheap features and report AUC over agreement alone. Expected information value medium: agreement carries almost all the signal (AUC 0.914 alone, 0.923 with all cheap features), so the likely result is another kill, which would fit claim 3 not supported. A pass would be the first sign of headroom for the learned arm.

Critic note (G01, 2026-10-18). Checked every number in both G01 blocks against runs/G01/result.json, table.md, run.log and diag_threshold_cold/result.json. Reran exp/g01_label_free_baselines.py on CPU into runs/G01_critic/ (parsed result.json identical to runs/G01/result.json, gate passes with diff 0.000). Reimplemented fixed_agreement_threshold and recal_threshold independently in plain Python: per-seed hits are identical at all four budgets. No leakage found: the six features come from rollout answers and lengths, gold enters only through labels of groups already verified (greedy and recal) and through the fit-split classifier used only by the reference-only static column. All policies spend exactly 109 / 218 / 545 / 1,090 calls. run.log has no warnings or errors and done.flag reads exit 0. Rule check: greedy minus recal is -25.57, -2.64, +0.89, -0.02 pp, so the pass rule fails, no gap lies in the 3 to 5 pp band, and the kill rule holds at all four budgets. The KILL verdict stands. Corrections, none of which changes a verdict: (1) "Recal wins at 5% (+2.5 pp over fixed)" was subtracted from rounded table entries; the raw hits give 85.55 vs 83.14, which is +2.4 pp. (2) The Claim 2 bullet "below recal at 5%" understates the result: greedy is also below recal at 10% (-2.64 pp, lower in 6 of 8 seeds), and at 25% and 50% it is within +0.9 pp of recal. (3) "label-free" for recal_threshold means no pre-labeled training split and no trained model. It does consume the verified gold labels (per-stratum error table refit every R verified labels), the same label stream greedy_online sees, which is what makes it a fair comparator, but it is not zero-label. (4) The seed sd is population sd (ddof 0) over 8 seeds; with ddof 1 the 2x max-sd bars are 8.1 / 5.3 / 1.4 / 1.0 instead of 7.6 / 5.0 / 1.3 / 1.0, and no outcome changes. Robustness beyond the ledger (the L1a question, CPU only, output in runs/G01_critic/split_robustness.txt): on 16 fit/sim split seeds (shuffle seeds 0 to 15, K = 1, 8 tie-break seeds each) the G01 rule gives KILL on every split. The largest greedy-minus-recal gap at 10% or 25% is +2.91 pp (split 14, 25%), and splits 13 and 14 (+2.68, +2.91 at 25%) come within 0.1 to 0.3 pp of the 3 pp kill boundary, so the 25% margin is thin even though no split nears the 5 pp pass bar. The 10% gap is -2.64 pp on the E01 split, the most favorable to greedy of the 16, and runs from -5.5 to -14.1 pp on the others. Corrected verdict: KILL (unchanged).

## L1b 2026-10-18 pass (tuned label-free rule beats plain fixed at 5% and 10%, one rollout pool)
Hypothesis: a label-free rule tuned on split seeds 0 to 3 (prior strength k0, refit cadence R, stratum grid) beats the plain fixed agreement threshold by more than 2 pp of hit rate on held-out split seeds 4 to 7 at some budget. Null: no tuned rule does.
Run: avrl/exp/l1b_tuned_threshold.py, a copy of G01 cut to the plain fixed threshold, the G01 reference recal, and a 27-config recal sweep (k0 in {2, 10, 50}; R = max(budget // d, 10) for d in {16, 8, 4}; stratum agreement, agreement x n_distinct, or agreement x n_distinct x any_truncated). K = 1, CPU only, 71 s on 8 processes. Files: avrl/runs/L1b/ (result.json, table.md, run.log, done.flag). Gates passed on split seed 0: plain fixed matches G01 at all four budgets (diff 0.0000 pp, per seed identical), and reference recal matches G01 per seed (zero hit difference).

Held-out (split seeds 4 to 7, 8 tie-break seeds each), hit rate in %:

| budget | selected config (chosen on seeds 0 to 3) | plain fixed | reference recal | selected | gap mean (pp) | sd over 4 splits | gap per split (4, 5, 6, 7) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 5% | k0 2, R = max(b//8, 10), agreement x n_distinct | 83.9 | 84.8 | 87.9 | +3.96 | 1.51 | +2.65, +5.85, +2.83, +4.52 |
| 10% | k0 50, R = max(b//8, 10), agreement x n_distinct x any_truncated | 69.7 | 70.5 | 75.1 | +5.38 | 0.41 | +5.27, +5.10, +5.14, +5.99 |
| 25% | k0 50, R = max(b//16, 10), agreement x n_distinct | 46.9 | 45.8 | 47.7 | +0.84 | 0.14 | +0.82, +0.92, +0.95, +0.65 |
| 50% | k0 50, R = max(b//16, 10), agreement x n_distinct | 27.9 | 27.8 | 28.2 | +0.24 | 0.10 | +0.20, +0.29, +0.36, +0.14 |

Sample sizes: 75 buckets and 2,180 to 2,315 sim docs on tune splits 0 to 3, 2,186 to 2,389 on held-out splits 4 to 7, 303 to 362 proxy errors per split. Verifier calls on split 0: 109, 218, 545, 1,090. On held-out splits: 109 to 119, 218 to 238, 546 to 597, 1,093 to 1,194.

Verdict: PASS by the pre-registered rule. Two budgets clear +2.0 pp on held-out (5% and 10%), and the gap is positive on all four held-out splits at both, so it is not a fragile pass by the definition used. The bar for claim 2 at 5% and 10% becomes the tuned rule. At 25% and 50% no tuned rule clears the bar, so plain fixed stays the bar there.

What it changes:
- Claim 2 at 5% and 10%: the comparator is now the tuned rule, which sits above plain fixed (held-out 87.9 vs 83.9 at 5%; 75.1 vs 69.7 at 10%). The learned arm was already below plain fixed on split 0 (60.0 vs 83.1 at 5%; 63.2 vs 72.0 at 10%), so claim 2 stays unsupported at 5% and 10%, by a wider margin.
- Claim 2 at 25% and 50%: the bar barely moves (tuned rule within +0.9 pp of plain fixed). The learned arm was at parity with plain fixed at 25% on split 0 (46.4 vs 46.3). It was not run on the held-out splits, so 25% stays undetermined.
- Where the gain comes from (sweep table in avrl/runs/L1b/table.md). At 5% it is refit cadence: the G01 reference (k0 2, agreement x n_distinct) uses R = 40 at this budget and gains +0.89 pp, while the same rule with R = 10 gains +4.94 pp in hindsight, R = 13 gains +3.96 and R = 27 gains +2.10. Prior strength barely matters at 5%. At 10% prior strength matters: with R = 27 and the agreement x n_distinct x any_truncated stratum, k0 = 2 gives +0.53 pp and k0 = 50 gives +5.38 pp. Stratum agreement alone is 0 to -4 pp at 5% and -3 to -12 pp at 10%, so the n_distinct split carries the gain.

Caveats:
1. Held-out is held out only for the choice of config. Every held-out sim document is also a sim document in at least one tune split (100%), and about 25% are in all four. All eight splits draw from one GSM8K train rollout pool (3,000 prompts, Qwen2.5-1.5B, 8 rollouts, cell100 buckets). The sd over four splits therefore does not measure pool-level noise. Treat the PASS as established within this pool only.
2. Selection ties and plateau. At 5% k0 = 2, 10 and 50 tie on tune at 88.40, and the sweep order picks k0 = 2. At 10% the pick (75.54 on tune) sits within 0.3 pp of two other k0 = 50 configs with the same stratum (75.27, 75.50).
3. The 5% gain uses the R floor of 10, which is a deliberate change from the G01 reference floor of 40 (see Run above). Under the reference floor the 5% gap is +0.89 pp.
4. Not zero-label. Recal and every tuned rule read verified gold labels through per-stratum counts. "Label-free" means no trained scorer, as in G01.
5. Replay only, as in G01: one model, one task, majority-vote proxy, cheap features, no GRPO.

Next, not run here: (a) replicate the 5% and 10% gains on a fresh rollout pool (needs new rollouts on the GPU through gpu_run.sh); (b) compute the learned arm (greedy_online) on held-out splits 4 to 7 so claim 2 can be compared with the tuned bar on the same splits (CPU).

Backlog: L1a marked done (critic run on 16 split seeds, KILL on 16 of 16 splits, not rerun). L1b status line added to lit/avrl/EXPERIMENT_BACKLOG.md.

## L1b 2026-10-18 pass

Verification entry. The L1b entry above was written by the run step. This entry rechecks its numbers against avrl/runs/L1b/result.json, table.md and run.log, and against the sim split membership recomputed from runs/p0/feats.parquet. No headline number changes. Two things are added: the document overlap between tune and held-out splits, and the exact tie at 5%.

Hypothesis: a label-free rule tuned on split seeds 0 to 3 (prior strength k0, refit cadence R, stratum grid) beats the plain fixed agreement threshold by more than 2 pp of hit rate on held-out split seeds 4 to 7 at some budget. Null: no tuned rule does.

What was run: avrl/exp/l1b_tuned_threshold.py, CPU only, 71 s on 8 processes, no GPU job. Replay of the Qwen2.5-1.5B GSM8K rollouts in avrl/runs/p0/feats.parquet (3,000 prompts, 8 rollouts, majority-vote proxy vs gold). Sim splits: 75 cell100 buckets each, 2,180 to 2,315 sim docs on tune splits 0 to 3, 2,186 to 2,389 on held-out splits 4 to 7. Budgets 5, 10, 25 and 50% of sim docs (109, 218, 545, 1,090 calls on split 0). Seeds: split seeds 0 to 7, 8 tie-break seeds each, K = 1. Sweep: 27 configs (k0 in {2, 10, 50}, R = max(b//d, 10) for d in {16, 8, 4}, three stratum keys), plus the G01 reference recal kept as a separate row.

Reproduction gates on split 0 pass: plain fixed gives 83.14, 71.96, 46.33 and 27.28 at 5, 10, 25 and 50%, identical per seed to G01. Reference recal is identical per seed to G01.

Hit rate (%), held-out split seeds 4 to 7, mean over the 8 tie-break seeds per split; the gap is paired by tie-break seed:

| budget | selected config (chosen on seeds 0 to 3) | plain fixed | reference recal | selected | gap mean (pp) | sd over 4 splits | gap per split, 4 / 5 / 6 / 7 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 5% | k0 2, R = max(b//8, 10), agreement x n_distinct | 83.93 | 84.82 | 87.89 | +3.96 | 1.51 | +2.65 / +5.85 / +2.83 / +4.52 |
| 10% | k0 50, R = max(b//8, 10), agreement x n_distinct x any_truncated | 69.74 | 70.54 | 75.12 | +5.38 | 0.41 | +5.27 / +5.10 / +5.14 / +5.99 |
| 25% | k0 50, R = max(b//16, 10), agreement x n_distinct | 46.87 | 45.84 | 47.71 | +0.84 | 0.14 | +0.82 / +0.92 / +0.95 / +0.65 |
| 50% | k0 50, R = max(b//16, 10), agreement x n_distinct | 27.94 | 27.83 | 28.18 | +0.24 | 0.10 | +0.20 / +0.29 / +0.36 / +0.14 |

Verification done here:
- Selection and statistics recomputed from result.json. For each budget the selected config is the argmax of the tune mean over the 27 configs, and the held-out mean and sd (ddof 1) from the per-split gaps match the stored values.
- The 5% selection is an exact three-way tie on tune (88.40): k0 2, 10 and 50, each with agreement x n_distinct and R = max(b//8, 10). The script keeps k0 2 (first in sweep order). Held-out gaps for the three are +3.96 (k0 2), +4.12 (k0 10) and +4.12 (k0 50). The verdict does not move, but the named config at 5% depends on the tie break.
- Overlap, recomputed from feats.parquet with the same bucket shuffle and fit cut: every held-out sim doc (split seeds 4 to 7) is also a sim doc in at least one tune split (100%). Between 24% and 28% of each held-out split is also in all four tune splits (0.271, 0.250, 0.242, 0.284). Across all eight splits, 2,999 distinct prompts appear.
- Run log sizes, proxy error counts (303 to 362 on held-out) and verifier call counts match result.json.

Verdict: PASS by the pre-registered rule. At 5% (+3.96 pp) and 10% (+5.38 pp) the held-out gap is above +2.0 pp, and it is positive on all four held-out splits at both budgets, so it is not a fragile pass by the rule's definition. Its weight is limited: the held-out splits are held out only for config choice, they draw from the same single rollout pool as the tune splits, and the 4-split sd does not measure pool-level noise. Read it as: within this pool, a tuned label-free rule beats plain fixed at 5% and 10%.

What it changes:
- Claim 2 (learned allocator admits fewer corrupted rewards than any fixed rule at equal budget). At 5% and 10% the bar becomes the tuned rule (held-out 87.9 and 75.1), not plain fixed (83.9 and 69.7). The learned arm (greedy online, G01, split 0) was already below plain fixed at both budgets (60.0 and 63.2 against 83.1 and 72.0), so it is further below the new bar. At 25% and 50% the tuned rule is within +0.9 pp of plain fixed, so plain fixed stays the bar there. The learned arm has not been run on held-out splits 4 to 7, so its 25% result against the bar is undetermined. Current reading: not_supported.
- Claim 3 (edge comes from structure in prompt space). L1b puts no prompt-space feature in any stratum: cell100 is used only to cut the splits. The tuned rule's edge comes from cheap features (n_distinct as a stratum key), refit cadence and prior strength. Together with E01 (embedding AUC 0.606 alone, neighbor ratio about 1.0) and G01, the current reading is not_supported on this task and proxy pair. L1b alone does not test prompt space.
- Scope unchanged: one model, one task, majority-vote proxy, cheap features only, replay only, no GRPO. "Label-free" here means no trained scorer. The tuned rule reads verified labels through per-stratum counts, as recal does.

Follow-ups (ids in lit/avrl/EXPERIMENT_BACKLOG.md under "Proposed by the loop"):
1. L2a, disjoint held-out pool (CPU, about 15 min). Expected information value high. Assign the cell100 groups to two disjoint pools, tune and held-out, with no document in both, run the same fit cut inside each, and swap roles. This removes the overlap caveat above and gives the learned arm a held-out number at 25%.
2. L2b, fresh rollout pool (one GPU job through gpu_run.sh, about 40 min at 2,000 to 3,000 prompts per the compute note). Expected information value highest for claim 2, since it is the only test here that separates the 5% and 10% gains from pool-level noise. Fix the configs from L1b before the run and do not retune on the new pool. Start at 2,000 prompts to stay clear of the 45-minute cap.
3. L2c, prompt-space stratum for the tuned rule (CPU, about 10 min). Add a coarse embedding cell (cell30) to the stratum key and rerun the sweep at 5 to 50%. Expected information value medium for claim 3. E01 and G01 predict no gain, so a kill is the likely result, and a pass would be the first prompt-space signal on this pair.

Critic note (L1b, 2026-10-18). Checked both L1b entries against runs/L1b/result.json, table.md, run.log, exit_code, done.flag and exp/l1b_tuned_threshold.py. Reran the script on CPU into runs/L1b_critic/ (rerun.log): result.json and table.md are byte-identical to runs/L1b/ (same md5). Reimplemented the plain fixed threshold and the swept recal rule in plain Python (exp/l1b_critic_indep.py, runs/L1b_critic/indep.log) for the two selected configs on split seeds 4 to 7: per-seed hits give held-out gaps +3.960 pp (5%) and +5.376 pp (10%) with sd 1.515 and 0.414, identical to the stored values. Every table number, sample size, call count, gate result, tune selection (argmax over the 27 configs, with the exact float tie at 5% as stated), sd (ddof 1) and the G01 learned-arm figures quoted in the entry (greedy 59.98 / 63.19 / 46.35 against fixed 83.14 / 71.96 / 46.33 at 5 / 10 / 25%) match the raw files. The overlap numbers are reproduced (100% of each held-out split is in at least one tune split; 0.271, 0.250, 0.242, 0.284 are in all four; 2,999 distinct prompts). No leakage: agreement, n_distinct and any_truncated come from rollout answers and lengths, the Beta prior is 1 - agreement, and gold (proxy_error) reaches an allocator only through documents already verified inside the budget; the unused cheap-feature list is never read, and the gold-derived pass_rate column in feats.parquet is not read. Budget is int(f * n_sim) for every policy on a split, recal stops at exactly that many calls, and fixed and tuned share the per-seed random draw, so the pairing is exact. run.log has no warnings or errors, ends with the wrote line, and exit_code reads 0. Rule check: selected-config gaps are +3.96, +5.38, +0.84, +0.24 pp at 5 / 10 / 25 / 50%, so the PASS rule (above +2.0 pp at some budget) holds at 5% and 10% and the KILL rule does not apply. The pass is not fragile under either reading of "positive": all four held-out splits are above +2.0 pp at 5% (minimum +2.65) and at 10% (minimum +5.10). No config in the 27-config sweep clears +2.0 pp at 25% (max +0.84) or 50% (max +0.24). In-pool seed noise is small (8 tie-break seeds, about 0.5 pp on the 4-split mean).

Correction (does not change the verdict): the refit cadences quoted in the "where the gain comes from" bullet (R = 13 and R = 27 at 5%, R = 27 at 10%) are split 0 values (budgets 109 and 218). On the held-out splits R is 13 to 14 for d = 8 and 27 to 29 for d = 4 at 5%, and 27 to 29 for d = 8 at 10%. The R = 10 floor and R = 40 for the reference recal are exact.

Robustness beyond the ledger (the L2a question, CPU only; exp/l1b_critic_disjoint.py, runs/L1b_critic/disjoint.json and disjoint.log): 8 random 49 / 50 partitions of the 99 cell100 groups, both directions, 16 runs. The full 27-config sweep (same Sim class and SPECS as the script under review) is tuned on 4 sub-splits of one pool, and the winner per budget is scored against plain fixed on 4 sub-splits of the other pool. No document is in both pools. Sub-splits have about 1,100 docs, so 5% is about 55 calls and part of the spread is small-pool noise. Held-out gap, mean / sd over the 16 runs / runs above +2.0 pp / runs above 0: 5%: +2.69 / 1.84 / 9 of 16 / 16 of 16. 10%: +4.68 / 1.74 / 15 of 16 / 16 of 16. 25%: +0.32 / 0.60 / 0 / 9 of 16. 50%: +0.32 / 0.17 / 0 / 16 of 16. Reading: the 10% gain survives disjoint tune and evaluation documents, and the winners are k0 = 50 configs with an n_distinct stratum, as in L1b. The 5% gain stays positive in every run but shrinks to about +2.7 pp and is below +2.0 pp in 7 of 16 runs, so at 5% the pass is thin and its margin over the bar is not established beyond the overlapping L1b splits. 25% and 50% still do not pass.

Corrected verdict: PASS by the pre-registered rule (unchanged). Strength: solid at 10%; thin at 5% (L1b held-out +3.96 pp, disjoint-pool mean +2.69 pp). Bar for claim 2: the tuned rule at 10% (held-out 75.1); at 5% carry plain fixed 83.9 and tuned 87.9 as the range until L2b; plain fixed at 25% and 50%. The learned arm has still not been run on held-out splits. Pool-level replication (L2b) remains the decisive test.

## L2b 2026-10-18 pass

Hypothesis: the L1b held-out gains of the label-free tuned rule over plain fixed (+3.96 pp at 5%, +5.38 pp at 10%) reproduce on a fresh GSM8K rollout pool disjoint from the L1b pool, with the two configs fixed from L1b and no retuning. Null: both gaps are at or below +2.0 pp.

What was run:
- Rollouts: avrl/exp/l2b_rollouts_fresh.py, a copy of p0_rollouts.py. Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct, G = 8, temperature 1.0, top_p 1.0, max_tokens 512, generation seed 1. Prompts: 3,000 GSM8K train problems sampled with random_state 1 from the 4,473 train rows outside the L1b pool. Question-text overlap with the L1b pool (avrl/runs/p0/feats.parquet): 0 of 3,000. One GPU job through gpu_run.sh, exit 0, generation 1,097 s.
- Features: avrl/exp/l2b_feats.py, the six cheap features and the cell30, cell100 and cell300 KMeans cells of p0_analyze.py.

- Pool: 24,000 rollouts. Majority-proxy error rate 15.3% of groups (L1b pool 14.5%). Majority accuracy 84.7%, rollout accuracy 63.6%. Sim splits of 2,196 to 2,369 docs, 75 cell100 buckets each, 330 to 374 proxy errors. Verifier calls per split: 109 to 118 at 5%, 219 to 236 at 10%.
- Files: avrl/runs/L2b/ (result.json, table.md, summary.json, rollouts.parquet, groups.parquet, feats.parquet, replay/).

| B: k0 50, R = max(b//8, 10), agreement x n_distinct x any_truncated | 10% | 68.52 | 73.65 | +5.13 | 0.96 | +4.09 to +6.67 | 8 of 8 | 8 of 8 |

Verdict: pass, by the pre-registered rule. Both pre-fixed configs clear +2.0 pp in mean and on all eight splits. The kill rule does not apply, and no inconclusive outcome arises.

Gates: exit code 0. The spend gate passes (3,712 of 3,712 checks; every policy x budget x seed spends int(f x n_sim) calls). The replay prints no errors.

Deviations from the plan:
1. The unchanged avrl/exp/l1b_tuned_threshold.py exits 3 on this pool at its G01 reproduction gate ("GATE FAILED: tuning and held-out steps not run"). That gate compares against the E01 reference (avrl/runs/G01), so it cannot match on a fresh pool. The replay therefore uses avrl/exp/l2b_replay.py, which replaces only that gate with the spend-count gate. The unchanged run's output is in avrl/runs/L2b/replay/unchanged_run.log.
2. The GPU log has "EngineCore died unexpectedly" after the generation engine shut down. The rollouts and summary were already written, the feature step after it completed, and the job exit code is 0. Treated as shutdown noise.

What it changes:
- Claim 2 at 5% and 10%: the tuned rule's gain over plain fixed replicates on a second rollout pool, at both budgets and on all eight splits. The bar for claim 2 at 5% and 10% stays the tuned rule (85.3 against 80.8 at 5%; 73.7 against 68.5 at 10%), now seen on two pools. This raises the comparator; it is not a learned-arm result.
- Claim 2 as stated (a learned allocator admits fewer corrupted rewards than any fixed rule at equal budget): unchanged, not_supported on this task and proxy pair. The learned arm (greedy_online, G01) was below plain fixed at 5% and 10% on split 0 of the L1b pool (59.98 against 83.14; 63.19 against 71.96). It has not been run on the fresh pool or on held-out splits 4 to 7, so the 25% bar is still undetermined.
- Claim 3 (the edge comes from structure in prompt space): unchanged, not_supported on this pair. The gains use cheap rollout features only (agreement, n_distinct, any_truncated). No prompt-space feature enters a stratum, so L2b does not test prompt space. L2c still does.
- Scope unchanged: one model, one task, majority-vote proxy, cheap features, replay only, no GRPO. This is a second pool, not a distribution of pool effects. The eight splits are overlapping fit and sim cuts of one pool, so the sd does not measure pool-level noise.

The L1b pool gave +0.89 pp at 5% under this floor. Expected information value high: it tests whether the 5% gain depends on the deliberate floor change, the weakest part of the 5% claim.
2. L3b, learned arm on held-out splits 4 to 7 of both pools, with 25% and 50% added (CPU, about 15 min). On split 0 of the L1b pool the learned arm sits 23 pp below plain fixed at 5%, so a kill is the likely result.

Critic note (L2b, 2026-10-18). Checked the L2b entry against runs/L2b/result.json, table.md, summary.json, replay/replay.json, replay/run.log, replay/unchanged_run.log, run.log, exit_code, done.flag, cmd.sh and the scripts exp/l2b_rollouts_fresh.py, l2b_feats.py and l2b_replay.py. Every number in the entry matches the raw files: pool statistics (24,000 rollouts, majority-proxy error 15.33%, majority accuracy 84.67%, rollout accuracy 63.65%), split sizes (2,196 to 2,369 docs, 75 buckets, 330 to 374 proxy errors, 109 to 118 and 219 to 236 calls), per-split gaps, means and sds (A +4.522, sd 1.190; B +5.130, sd 0.961, ddof 1), hit rates (80.82 / 85.34 at 5%; 68.52 / 73.65 at 10%), the splits 4 to 7 figures (+5.03, +4.97), the L1b rows, the L2a rows (+2.69 / 1.84 / 9 of 16; +4.68 / 1.74 / 15 of 16) and the G01 learned-arm figures (59.98 / 63.19 against 83.14 / 71.96). Independent checks (exp/l2b_critic_indep.py, runs/L2b_critic/indep.log): agreement, n_distinct, any_truncated, the majority and proxy_error rebuilt from rollouts.parquet all match groups.parquet; question overlap with p0 is 0 of 3,000 and gold equals the dataset answer; plain fixed and configs A and B reimplemented in plain Python (own loop, no import of the L1b module) and replayed on splits 0 to 7 with 8 tie-break seeds give per-split gaps equal to result.json (max diff 4e-15 pp), and every policy spends exactly int(f * n_sim) calls. The replay wrapper reproduces the stored L1b per-split gaps exactly on the L1b pool (exp/l2b_critic_wrapper.py, wrapper.log, max diff 0.0). Configs A and B are the L1b selections in runs/L1b/result.json and match the backlog text. No leakage: the allocator reads agreement, n_distinct, any_truncated and verified labels only, the prior is 1 - agreement, cell100 (unsupervised KMeans on question embeddings) only cuts the splits, and proxy_error reaches a policy only for docs already verified inside the budget. The rollout script differs from p0_rollouts.py only in ROOT, seed default, output dir and the prompt draw plus its disjointness asserts. run.log shows no error except the EngineCore line, which comes after "Shutdown complete" and after rollouts, summary and feats were written (exit_code 0). Truncation is not hidden: 2.5% of rollouts hit 512 tokens and 13.2% of prompts have at least one (p0: 2.2% and 12.1%). The rule is applied correctly: both mean gaps are above +2.0 pp, so PASS, and pass (either above) and kill (both at or below) are complementary, so no inconclusive band exists.

Correction (strength, verdict unchanged): the entry says the 5% gain "that the L2a critic left thin ... is +4.52 here" and that the gain replicates at both budgets. The point estimates pass, but the sd over the 8 splits (1.19, 0.96) is cut-to-cut, not pool-level (the entry says so), and pool-level noise is about as large as the 5% margin over +2.0. Measured on CPU (exp/l2b_critic_noise.py and l2b_critic_splits64.py; runs/L2b_critic/noise.log, splits64.log): (a) 12 random partitions of the 100 cell100 buckets into two disjoint halves (about 1,500 docs, 75 calls at 5%), 24 half-pool runs, same configs, 8 tie-break seeds: A at 5% mean +3.82, sd 2.92, 22 of 24 above 0, 17 of 24 above +2.0, min -1.30; B at 10% mean +5.12, sd 1.95, 24 of 24 above +2.0, min +2.10. (b) Bucket bootstrap (200 resamples of 75 buckets with replacement, 4 tie-break seeds; duplicated buckets inflate the spread): A 95% interval -0.23 to +10.87, 153 of 200 above +2.0; B +1.17 to +9.83, 189 of 200 above +2.0. (c) 64 random 75-of-100 bucket cuts give a cut-to-cut sd of 1.52 (A) and 1.25 (B), larger than the 1.19 and 0.96 over splits 0 to 7; a rough delete-d jackknife scale (sqrt(3) times the cut sd) puts the pool-level sd at about 2.6 (A) and 2.2 (B), so the mean gaps sit about 1.1 and 1.4 sd above +2.0. The L2a comparison (+2.69, 9 of 16) used pools of about 1,100 docs and tuned winners, not these pre-fixed configs on 2,200+ docs, so +4.52 against +2.69 is not evidence that the 5% gain has stopped being thin. Minor: the file list omits replay_unchanged/result.json, the GATE_FAILED record of the unchanged L1b script.

Corrected verdict: PASS by the pre-registered rule (unchanged). Strength: B at 10% replicated on a second pool and is above +2.0 on all 24 disjoint half-pools; A at 5% is a point-estimate pass (+4.52, positive on 22 of 24 half-pools) whose margin over +2.0 is not separated from pool-level noise, so carry it as thin. For claim 2, carry the 5% bar as a range (tuned 85.3 to 87.9, plain fixed 80.8 to 83.9 across the two pools) and the 10% bar as the tuned rule (73.7 here, 75.1 on the L1b pool). The not_supported readings of claims 2 and 3 are unchanged. A third pool (L3c) is the test that would settle the 5% margin.

## L3a 2026-10-18 pass

Hypothesis: the label-free 5% gain of config A (k0 2, stratum agreement x n_distinct, refit cadence R = max(budget // 8, floor)) survives moving the refit floor from 10 (L2b) to 40 (the G01 reference), on the fresh GSM8K rollout pool (avrl/runs/L2b). Null: at floor 40 the fresh-pool mean gap over plain fixed is at or below +2.0 pp, so the 5% gain is a refit-cadence effect rather than a property of the label-free rule.

What was run:
- Script: avrl/exp/l3a_floor_sensitivity.py, a copy of avrl/exp/l2b_replay.py. The only change is the refit floor (40). l2b_replay.py and l1b_tuned_threshold.py are untouched. Policies: plain fixed threshold, config A at 5% (k0 2, agreement x n_distinct), config B at 10% (k0 50, agreement x n_distinct x any_truncated). Split seeds 0 to 7, 8 tie-break seeds per split, gap paired by seed. No retuning, no argmax. CPU only, no GPU job.
- Model and task as in L2b: Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct rollouts, GSM8K, majority-vote proxy vs gold, cheap features. Fresh pool: 24,000 rollouts. Sim splits of 2,196 to 2,369 docs, 75 cell100 buckets, 330 to 374 proxy errors. Verifier calls per split: 109 to 118 at 5%, 219 to 236 at 10%.
- Context run: the same script on the L1B pool (avrl/runs/p0/feats.parquet), the pool on which L1b tuned the configs. Verdict CONTEXT_ONLY.
- Files: avrl/runs/L3a/fresh/ and avrl/runs/L3a/l1b_pool/ (result.json, table.md, run.log, replay/replay.json), avrl/runs/L3a/result.json (summary), exit_code 0, done.flag.

Results on the fresh pool, hit rate in %, gap in pp, means over split seeds 0 to 7:

| config | budget | plain fixed (%) | config (%) | gap mean (pp) | sd (ddof 1) | gap range (pp) | splits above 0 | splits above +2.0 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A: k0 2, agreement x n_distinct, R = max(b//8, 40) | 5% | 80.82 | 83.98 | +3.16 | 3.01 | 0.00 to +7.72 | 5 of 8 | 5 of 8 |
| B: k0 50, agreement x n_distinct x any_truncated, R = max(b//8, 40) | 10% | 68.52 | 73.54 | +5.02 | 1.00 | +3.64 to +6.73 | 8 of 8 | 8 of 8 |

Per split, fresh pool, floor 40 (gap in pp, A at 5% / B at 10%): split 0 0.00 / +4.22; split 1 +2.54 / +5.86; split 2 +5.86 / +4.84; split 3 0.00 / +6.73; split 4 0.00 / +4.98; split 5 +5.33 / +5.57; split 6 +7.72 / +4.29; split 7 +3.83 / +3.64.

| run | config | budget | gap mean (pp) | sd | splits above +2.0 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| L2b fresh, floor 10, splits 0 to 7 | B | 10% | +5.13 | 0.96 | 8 of 8 |

Verification done here (CPU; nothing written outside avrl/runs/L3a):
- Means and sds recomputed from the per-split gaps in result.json: fresh A +3.159 (sd 3.012), fresh B +5.018 (sd 1.001), L1B pool A on splits 4 to 7 +0.888 (sd 1.78), B +5.18 (sd 0.75). All match the report.
- Each per-split gap equals the difference of the stored hit rates (max diff 1e-14 pp). Hit rates recomputed: 80.82 and 83.98 at 5%; 68.52 and 73.54 at 10%.
- Spend: int(f x n_sim) equals the stored verifier calls on all 16 fresh split-budget pairs. Spend gate in the run: 384 checks per pool, 0 failures.
- Reproduction gate: config A at 5% on L1B splits 4 to 7 gives [0.0, 3.555046, 0.0, 0.0], against [0.0, 3.555046, 0.0, 0.0] stored in avrl/runs/L1b/result.json. Max abs diff 0.000000 pp (tolerance 0.01). PASS.
- Script diff against l2b_replay.py: only the floor, the pool and result wiring, and the Gate B reproduction check differ. The policy functions and the spend gate are as in L2b.
- Zero-gap splits: recomputed in memory on fresh splits 0, 3 and 4 (per-seed hits, no file written). Config A at 5% makes exactly the same hits as plain fixed on all 8 tie-break seeds of each of these splits. Split 1 is not uniform: its per-seed hit differences are -1, 0, +7, 0, 0, +9, 0, +8.
- Mechanism: at 5% (109 to 118 calls) a floor of 40 gives R = 40, so the rule refits only 2 or 3 times. A floor of 10 gives R = 13 or 14, about 8 refits. Consistent with the zero gaps: 3 of 8 fresh splits and 5 of 8 L1B splits are exactly 0.00 at floor 40.

Verdict: pass, by the pre-registered rule. Config A at 5% has a fresh-pool mean gap of +3.16 pp over split seeds 0 to 7, above +2.0. The kill rule does not apply. The plan expected a kill at this floor, based on the L1B pool (+0.89 pp). That expectation did not hold on the fresh pool.

Strength: thin. The one-sample t against +2.0 is 1.09 (7 df). The 95% interval on the mean is +0.64 to +5.68, which includes +2.0. The mean rests on splits 2, 5 and 6 (+5.86, +5.33, +7.72); the other five splits average +1.27. The same floor on the other pool gives +0.89 (splits 4 to 7) and +1.30 (splits 0 to 7), below the bar. The 5% margin therefore passes on the fresh pool at floor 40 and falls below the bar on the L1B pool.

Gates: exit code 0. Spend gate passes on both pools. Reproduction gate passes at 0.000000 pp. Both gates passed before the verdict was read.

Deviations from the plan: none to the script. The L1B pool ran on all eight splits; its splits 0 to 7 figures are context only.

What it changes:
- Claim 2 at 5%: the 5% gain survives the floor change on the fresh pool, but at +3.16 against +4.52 at floor 10, with an interval that includes +2.0. On the L1B pool at the same floor it is below the bar. The 5% gain is a point estimate that depends on both the pool and the refit floor. Carry it as thin, and state the floor wherever the number appears. The tuned rule's 5% hit rate at floor 40 is 83.98 on the fresh pool (plain fixed 80.82), so the 5% bar on that pool falls from 85.3 (floor 10) to 84.0.
- Claim 2 at 10%: robust to the floor. Config B at 10% is +5.02 at floor 40 on the fresh pool (8 of 8 above +2.0; floor 10 gave +5.13), and +4.64 on splits 0 to 7 of the L1B pool (8 of 8). The 10% gain is the firmer part of claim 2. The 10% bar on the fresh pool at floor 40 is 73.54 against 68.52 for plain fixed.
- Claim 2 as stated (learned allocator against any fixed rule): unchanged, not_supported. L3a tests only the label-free rule, not the learned arm.
- Claim 3 (edge from prompt-space structure): unchanged, not_supported. Configs A and B use cheap rollout features, and cell100 only cuts the splits. L3a does not test claim 3.
- Scope unchanged: one model, one task, majority-vote proxy, cheap features, replay only, no GRPO. The fresh-pool verdict is one pool, and the two pools disagree at floor 40. The eight splits overlap in one pool, so the sd does not measure pool-level noise.

Follow-ups (ids in lit/avrl/EXPERIMENT_BACKLOG.md under "Proposed by the loop, iteration 4 (after L3a)"):
1. L4a, refit-count curve at 5% on both pools (CPU, about 10 min). Replace the floor with a fixed refit count per budget (about 2, 4, 8, 16 and 32 refits) on all splits of both pools, with no selection. Expected information value high for claim 2 at 5%. If the gain follows the refit count, the 5% claim is a cadence effect and must be stated as one. If it is flat in refit count, the floor is not the driver and the pool difference is the open question.
2. L4b, third rollout pool from the GSM8K test split (GPU, one job, about 10 min), with configs A and B at floors 10 and 40. Expected information value medium-high: the 5% margin now differs by pool at floor 40 (+3.16 against +0.89), so a third pool at both floors is the decisive test. About 1,300 prompts give 5% budgets near 50 calls per split, so the 5% read will be noisy and the 10% read is the firmer one.
3. L4c, learned arm on held-out splits 4 to 7 against both tuned bars (CPU, about 15 min; replaces the bar in L3b). Expected information value medium-high: claim 2 needs the learned arm against the tuned rule on the same splits, and that bar is now floor-dependent. A kill is still the likely result, since on split 0 of the L1B pool the learned arm sits 23 pp below plain fixed at 5% (59.98 against 83.14).


Critic note (L3a, 2026-10-18). Checked the L3a entry against runs/L3a/result.json, fresh/result.json, fresh/table.md, fresh/run.log, fresh/replay/replay.json, l1b_pool/result.json, l1b_pool/table.md, l1b_pool/run.log, summary.log, run.log, exit_code, done.flag, L3a.sh, and the scripts exp/l3a_floor_sensitivity.py and l3a_summary.py, plus runs/L1b/result.json and runs/L2b/result.json for the reference rows. Every number in the entry matches the raw files: per-split gaps, means and sds (fresh A +3.159 / sd 3.012, B +5.018 / sd 1.001; L1B pool A +0.889 / 1.78 on splits 4 to 7 and +1.299 / 1.87 on splits 0 to 7, B +5.18 / 0.75 and +4.64 / 0.77), counts above 0 and above +2.0 (5 of 8 and 5 of 8; 8 of 8), split sizes (2,196 to 2,369 docs, 75 buckets, 330 to 374 proxy errors, 109 to 118 and 219 to 236 calls), hit rates (80.82 and 83.98 at 5%; 68.52 and 73.54 at 10%), the floor-10 reference rows, the zero-gap counts (3 of 8 fresh, 5 of 8 L1B), the split 1 per-seed differences (-1, 0, +7, 0, 0, +9, 0, +8) and the t of 1.09 and interval +0.64 to +5.68. Independent checks (exp/l3a_critic_indep.py, runs/L3a_critic/indep.log): agreement, n_distinct, any_truncated and majority rebuilt from rollouts.parquet equal feats.parquet (max diff 0), proxy_error equals majority != gold on all 3,000 prompts, and the majority tie rule is lexicographic on the answer string, not gold-aware; plain fixed and configs A and B reimplemented in own code (no import of the L1b or L3a modules) and replayed at floor 40 on both pools, splits 0 to 7, 8 tie-break seeds, give per-split gaps equal to both result.json files (max diff 4e-15 pp), and every policy picks exactly int(f x n_sim) distinct docs. No leakage: the allocator keys are agreement, n_distinct, any_truncated and labels of docs already verified in budget; the prior is 1 - agreement; cell100 only cuts the splits. The L1B-pool reproduction gate passes at 0.000000 pp (config A at floor 40 is the G01 reference_recal rule, so the stored L1b values are the same function), the spend gate passes (384 checks per pool), the L1B pool ran first, exit code 0, and no error or truncation text appears in any log. The rule is applied correctly: fresh-pool mean gap +3.159 pp is above +2.0, so PASS, and pass and kill are complementary.

Corrections (verdict unchanged, strength and wording). (1) Mechanism: the entry says a floor of 40 refits "only 2 or 3 times" at 5%. It is exactly 2 on every split of both pools: every 5% budget is 109 to 119 calls, below 120, so refits fall after 40 and 80 verified labels only. The first 40 selections equal plain fixed by construction (the prior is the fixed rule's order and the tie-break draw is shared). Floor 10 gives 8 refits. On fresh splits 0, 3 and 4 the chosen set (not just the hit count) equals plain fixed's on all 8 seeds; split 1 on 4 of 8; splits 2 and 6 on none. (2) Strength is weaker than "thin" suggests. The t of 1.09 and the +0.64 to +5.68 interval treat the eight overlapping cuts of one pool as independent, which the entry's scope line already warns against. Measured on CPU (exp/l3a_critic_noise.py, runs/L3a_critic/noise.log), config A at 5% and floor 40 on the fresh pool: 64 random 75-of-100 bucket cuts give mean +3.20, sd 2.64, 21 of 64 exactly 0.00, 41 of 64 above +2.0, range 0.00 to +8.62; a rough delete-d jackknife scale (sqrt(3) times the cut sd) puts the pool-level sd near 4.6, so the mean sits about 0.26 sd above the +2.0 bar. The bucket bootstrap (200 resamples of 75 buckets, 4 seeds, duplicated buckets inflate the spread) gives mean +3.14, 95% interval 0.00 to +10.60, and only 99 of 200 resamples above +2.0. Relative to the bar the fresh-pool PASS is a coin flip at pool level. The same measures on the L1B pool give +1.86 (sd 2.07, 29 of 64 above +2.0, bootstrap 82 of 200), so +3.2 and +1.9 at floor 40 are not separated from each other or from +2.0. (3) The floor effect is separated on the L1B pool only. Floor 10 minus floor 40 for A at 5%: L1B pool +2.39 (sd 1.68, positive on 63 of 64 cuts); fresh pool +1.28 (sd 2.23, positive on 43 of 64 cuts; on the eight L3a splits +1.36, sd 2.77, t 1.39). So "depends on both the pool and the refit floor" is supported for the floor on the L1B pool, but on the fresh pool the loss from +4.52 to +3.16 is not distinguishable from cut noise, and the two pools disagreeing at floor 40 is a point-estimate difference, not a measured one. The 5% bar on the fresh pool is 84.0 (floor 40) to 85.3 (floor 10), carry it as that range. (4) Config B at 10% is the firm result, as the entry says: floor 40 fresh pool 63 of 64 cuts above +2.0 (mean +4.70, sd 1.25), bootstrap 187 of 200, 23 of 24 disjoint halves; L1B pool 62 of 64 cuts, bootstrap 188 of 200. Half-pool runs at 5% are not comparable (75 calls, one refit, 23 of 24 exactly 0.00 at floor 40 on the fresh pool), so they are not used. Minor: the file list omits summary.log.

Corrected verdict: PASS by the pre-registered rule (unchanged; both gates passed, mean +3.159 pp above +2.0). Strength: A at 5% with floor 40 is a point-estimate pass whose margin over +2.0 is not separated from pool-level noise, with the rule doing nothing at all (gap exactly 0.00) on about a third of fresh-pool cuts, so carry it as thin to coin-flip and state the floor wherever it appears. B at 10% is firm across floors and pools. Claims 2 and 3 readings (not_supported) are unchanged. L4a (refit-count curve) and L4b (third pool) are the right follow-ups; L4a should report cut-level spread across random bucket cuts, not only the eight L3a splits.

## L4b 2026-10-18 kill (5% claim), pass (10% claim)

Hypothesis: the 5% gain of config A and the 10% gain of config B reproduce on a third GSM8K rollout pool, disjoint from the L1B and L2b pools, with both configs fixed from L1b and no retuning. Null at refit floor 40: the mean gap over split seeds 0 to 7 is at or below +2.0 pp for each config. Each claim is judged separately: PASS if the mean is above +2.0 pp, else KILL.

What was run:
- Rollouts: Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct on GSM8K, G 8, temperature 1.0, top_p 1.0, max_tokens 512, generation seed 1. Script avrl/exp/l4b_rollouts_pool3.py (copy of l2b_rollouts_fresh.py, only the prompt draw changed). Proxy: majority vote of the 8 rollouts vs gold.
- Pool: 2,792 prompts (all 1,319 GSM8K test rows plus the 1,473 train rows left after the L1b and L2b draws), 22,336 rollouts. The script asserts zero question overlap with the L1B and L2b pools. Deviation from the L4b entry (test rows only, about 1,319 prompts), declared in the plan before the run: at that size the 5% budget is about 50 calls per split.
- Features: avrl/exp/l2b_feats.py, unchanged. Checked this session against the raw rollouts: agreement, n_distinct and any_truncated rebuilt from rollouts.parquet match feats.parquet, and proxy_error equals majority != gold on all 2,792 prompts.
- Replay: avrl/exp/l4b_replay.py, a copy of l3a_floor_sensitivity.py. Split seeds 0 to 7 (sims of 1,989 to 2,162 docs, 75 of 100 cell buckets), 8 tie-break seeds per split, gap paired by seed. Verifier calls per split: 99 to 108 at 5%, 198 to 216 at 10%. Spend gate: 640 checks, 0 failures.
- Gates: reproduction on the L1B pool at floor 40 matches runs/L3a/l1b_pool/result.json to 0.000000 pp (A +1.2994, B +4.6401). GPU job exit 0, CPU replay exit 0.
- Secondary, not in the verdict: 64 random 75-of-100 bucket cuts at floor 40.
- Files: avrl/runs/L4b/ (result.json, table.md, summary.json, cuts64.json, replay/replay.json, replay/repro_l1b_pool.json, run.log, replay/replay.log, replay/cuts.log, exit_code, replay_exit_code).

Numbers (hit rate in %, gap in pp against plain fixed at the same budget, 8 splits, split seeds 0 to 7 per split):

| policy | budget | refit floor | hit rate, mean over splits | gap mean (pp) | gap sd (pp) | splits above +2.0 | verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| plain fixed threshold | 5% | n/a | 92.06 | baseline | n/a | n/a | n/a |
| A: k0 2, agreement x n_distinct | 5% | 40 (verdict) | 92.06 | +0.00 | 0.00 | 0 of 8 | KILL |
| A | 5% | 10 (reference) | 92.70 | +0.65 | 0.97 | 1 of 8 | n/a |
| plain fixed threshold | 10% | n/a | 77.60 | baseline | n/a | n/a | n/a |
| B: k0 50, agreement x n_distinct x any_truncated | 10% | 40 (verdict) | 82.61 | +5.01 | 1.35 | 8 of 8 | PASS |
| B | 10% | 10 (reference) | 83.28 | +5.67 | 1.42 | 8 of 8 | n/a |

Per split at floor 40: the A 5% gap is 0.00 on all 8 splits. The B 10% gaps are +3.73, +3.71, +5.22, +7.86, +4.61, +5.43, +4.17, +5.32. Secondary cut level: A 5% at floor 40 is 0.00 on all 64 cuts; B 10% has mean +5.16, sd 1.38, and 62 of 64 cuts above +2.0.

Three pools at refit floor 40 (gap in pp, mean over split seeds 0 to 7):

| pool | A at 5% | B at 10% |
|---|---|---|
| L2b (the fresh pool in L3a) | +3.16 | +5.02 |
| L1B (p0) | +1.30 | +4.64 |
| L4b (this run, 2,792 prompts) | +0.00 | +5.01 |

Mechanism check: at floor 40, A refits exactly twice per split (after 40 and 80 verified labels), and its selected set equals plain fixed's on all 8 seeds of all 8 splits. This session I re-ran the independent Pool replay in avrl/exp/l3a_critic_indep.py by import, with no file written. It reproduces every per-split gap in result.json exactly, with 0 spend failures. Floor 10 refits about 8 times and gives +0.65.

Verdict against the pre-registered rule:
- Config A at 5%, floor 40: mean +0.00 pp, at or below +2.0, 0 of 8 splits above. KILL.
- Config B at 10%, floor 40: mean +5.01 pp, above +2.0 on 8 of 8 splits. PASS.
- Overall: kill for the 5% claim and pass for the 10% claim. The 5% claim was the open question this run was designed to settle, and it does not hold on a third pool.

What it changes:
- Claim 2 at 5%: the L3a pass does not reproduce. At floor 40 the 5% gain across the three pools is +3.16, +1.30 and +0.00 (mean +1.5, below +2.0). Do not report the 5% margin as supported. The floor-10 reference is +0.65 on this pool, so refit cadence does not rescue it here.
- Claim 2 at 10%: the most stable result in the loop. Config B holds at +4.6 to +5.0 pp at floor 40 on all three pools, 8 of 8 splits each. It is a tuned, label-fed adaptive stratum rule, not the learned allocator that claim 2 names, so it supports the adaptive direction but not the claim as worded.
- Claim 2 as stated (a learned allocator admits fewer corrupted rewards than any fixed rule): not_supported, unchanged. The only learned arm tested (greedy_online, L3b) remains far below plain fixed at 5%.
- Claim 3 (the edge comes from structure in prompt space): not_supported, unchanged. Config B uses only rollout-side features (agreement, n_distinct, any_truncated). The cell100 clusters define only the split buckets and are not allocator inputs, so this run gives no evidence for prompt-space structure as the source of the edge.
- Scope: one model, one task, majority-vote proxy, cheap features, replay of recorded rollouts. The 2,792-prompt pool deviates from the test-only plan, declared before the run.

Follow-ups (backlog ids L5a to L5c, under "Proposed by the loop, iteration 5 (after L4b)"):
1. L5a, frozen-table control. Fit config B's stratum rates once on the L1B pool with no online refit, then apply that fixed ranking on the L2b and L4b pools at 10%. If it holds near +5 pp, the gain comes from a label-derived fixed ranking, and claim 2's "any fixed rule" comparison must be restated. If it falls to about 0, the online refit carries the gain. Expected information value high for the wording of claim 2. CPU, about 10 min, existing feats files.
2. L5b, truncation ablation. Config B without any_truncated (agreement x n_distinct, k0 50) at 10%, floor 40, on all three pools. If the gain holds, the generation-side truncation feature is not needed. If it drops, any_truncated is the carrier, and it is the nearest rollout-side signal to problem difficulty, which bears on claim 3. Expected information value medium-high. CPU, about 10 min.
3. L5c, learned arm on three pools (extends L4c). greedy_online against the tuned rule at floors 10 and 40 on the L2b, L1B and L4b pools, held-out splits 4 to 7. The tuned rule's 5% gap at floor 40 is zero on the L4b pool, so there the learned arm is tested against plain fixed alone. Expected information value medium-high for claim 2. A kill is still the likely result. CPU, about 20 min.


Critic note (L4b, 2026-10-18). Checked the L4b entry against runs/L4b/result.json, table.md, summary.json, cuts64.json, replay/replay.json, replay/repro_l1b_pool.json, replay/replay.log, replay/cuts.log, run.log, exit_code, replay_exit_code, done.flag, rollouts.parquet, groups.parquet, feats.parquet, exp/L4b.sh, exp/l4b_rollouts_pool3.py, exp/l4b_replay.py, exp/l4b_critic_cuts.py, exp/l2b_feats.py, the L1b Sim code, runs/L3a/{fresh,l1b_pool}/result.json for the pool rows, and lit/avrl/EXPERIMENT_BACKLOG.md with the snap/ copies of the backlog and ledger. Independent checks are exp/l4b_critic_indep.py, l4b_critic_subpools.py, l4b_critic_subcuts.py, l4b_critic_sizecontrol.py and l4b_critic_composition.py, with logs in runs/L4b_critic/ (indep.log, subpools.log, subcuts.log, sizecontrol.log, composition.log).

What checks out. Every number in the entry matches the raw files: per-split gaps (B +3.73, +3.71, +5.22, +7.86, +4.61, +5.43, +4.17, +5.32; A 0.00 on all 8), means and sds (A 0.00 / 0.00, B +5.006 / 1.346; floor 10 A +0.647 / 0.971, B +5.673 / 1.420), the table hit rates (fixed 92.06 and 77.60, A 92.06 and 92.70 at floors 40 and 10, B 82.61 and 83.28), split sizes (1,989 to 2,162 docs, 75 buckets), calls (99 to 108 at 5%, 198 to 216 at 10%), 640 spend checks with 0 failures, the reproduction gate at 0.000000 pp, the 64-cut secondary (A 0.00 on 64 of 64; B +5.16, sd 1.38, 62 of 64 above +2.0), the three-pool table (+3.16 / +5.02, +1.30 / +4.64, +0.00 / +5.01; B 8 of 8 splits above +2.0 on every pool) and the 3-pool mean of +1.49 for A. Own reimplementation (no import of the L1b, L4b or L3a-critic modules): agreement, n_distinct, any_truncated and the majority (lexicographic tie rule, 317 tied prompts, not gold-aware) rebuilt from rollouts.parquet equal groups.parquet and feats.parquet with 0 differences; proxy_error equals majority != gold on all 2,792 prompts; gold recomputed from the GSM8K parquet files equals groups.gold on all rows; pids 0 to 1318 are test rows and the rest train rows; zero question overlap with the p0 and L2b pools. Plain fixed, A and B replayed at floors 40 and 10 on splits 0 to 7 with 8 tie-break seeds reproduce every per-split gap and every hit rate in result.json exactly (max diff 0), every policy picks exactly int(f x n_sim) distinct docs (0 failures), and A's chosen set equals plain fixed's on 8 of 8 seeds of all 8 splits at floor 40 with exactly 2 refits (floor 10: 8 refits, set equal on 8, 4, 0, 1, 0, 8, 1, 3 seeds). No leakage: the allocator keys are agreement, n_distinct, any_truncated and labels of docs already verified in budget, the prior is 1 - agreement, cell100 only cuts the splits. The GPU job exited 0 and wrote all 22,336 rollouts and 2,792 feature rows. The one ERROR line in run.log ("Engine core proc EngineCore died unexpectedly, shutting down client", 21:07:52) comes after "Shutdown complete" and after summary.json was written, so it is teardown noise. 3.09% of rollouts hit the 512-token cap (15.3% of prompts have any_truncated, against 12.1% and 13.2% on the p0 and L2b pools) and 6 rollouts have an empty answer; these feed the any_truncated feature and do not hide a crash. The rule is applied correctly on the pool as run: A mean +0.00 is at or below +2.0, so KILL; B mean +5.01 is above +2.0, so PASS; floor 10 and the cut level did not enter the verdict.

Corrections (verdict by the rule unchanged; strength and wording changed, mostly for B).
(1) The B PASS depends on the pool deviation. The backlog entry registered the test split only (1,319 prompts); the run used 2,792 prompts, 53% of them remaining train rows. The deviation is not neutral for B. Stand-in replays on the test-only rows of this pool, same Pool code, floor 40, splits 0 to 7, 8 seeds, buckets taken from the joint cell100 clustering (the MiniLM model needed to re-cluster the test rows alone is not in a permitted directory): A 5% +0.00 (fixed 97.23% hit), B 10% +0.58 (sd 1.07; splits +1.42, -0.25, -0.66, +0.51, +1.14, +2.40, -0.62, +0.74; fixed 84.50, B 85.09). Over 64 random bucket cuts of the test-only rows B is +0.16 (sd 0.83, 2 of 64 above +2.0 at floor 40; +0.62 and 9 of 64 at floor 10); with cell30 +0.10, cell300 +0.28, and a TF-IDF KMeans 100 re-bucketing of the test rows -0.08. On the remaining-train rows alone, A 5% is +5.82 and B 10% is +9.56 at floor 40 (64 cuts: A +5.86, 60 of 64 above +2.0; B +8.99, 64 of 64). Part of this is call-count scale: random 1,319-prompt halves (6 each, floor 40) give B 10% averages of +2.58 on L4b (range +0.31 to +5.21, 4 of 6 above +2.0), +4.03 on L2b (5 of 6) and +2.90 on p0 (4 of 6), and A 5% averages of +0.22, +3.03 and +1.01. So at the registered size B 10% sits near the +2.0 bar with a wide spread, and on the registered rows themselves it is below it. Had the registered design been run, the B reading would most likely have been KILL (stand-in estimate, not the real run). The entry's "most stable result in the loop" and "firm across floors and pools" hold at 2,800 to 3,000 prompts (about 200 calls at 10%) and should not be carried to 1,300 prompts. The selected sets are not a test-row artifact: test rows are 58.4% of plain fixed's 10% set and 57.8% of B's.
(2) The 5% KILL is robust as a verdict but its reading is confounded by baseline saturation. A gap of 0.00 holds on all 8 splits, 64 of 64 cuts, and the test-only rows; a bucket bootstrap (100 resamples of 75 buckets with replacement, 3 seeds) gives A +0.30, 5 of 100 above +2.0. But this pool is harder than the earlier two: proxy error 21.4% (test rows 26.2%, remaining train rows 17.0%) against 14.5% (p0) and 15.3% (L2b), and 4.3% of docs have all 8 answers distinct against 3.4% on both earlier pools. Plain fixed's 5% set therefore holds 88.75 all-distinct docs out of 102.25 calls on average and hits 92.06%, leaving 7.9 pp of headroom against 16.4 pp (p0, 83.59) and 19.2 pp (L2b, 80.82). A's gain across the three pools (+3.16, +1.30, +0.00) falls as the fixed baseline rises (80.82, 83.59, 92.06), and A is +5.9 on the remaining-train rows of this very pool. "The 5% gain does not hold on a third pool" is true by the rule, but the data show a rule that adds nothing when the fixed 5% set is already saturated, not a measured zero effect on a comparable pool. The 3-pool mean of +1.5 averages pools with different headroom and should not be read as an effect size.
(3) Pool-level firmness of B at 10% is weaker than 8 of 8 suggests. The eight splits overlap in one pool. Bucket bootstrap (100 resamples of 75 buckets with replacement, 3 seeds, duplicated buckets inflate spread): B +4.91, sd 2.51, 95% interval -0.04 to +9.42, 88 of 100 above +2.0. This matches the earlier L3a critic reading for the L2b pool (187 of 200) in direction, with a somewhat lower rate here.
(4) Wording: "declared in the plan before the run" is not supported on disk. The backlog entry (lit/avrl/EXPERIMENT_BACKLOG.md, section L4b) still says test rows only, and the backlog and ledger snapshots taken before 20:52 contain no mention of 2,792 or the remaining train rows. The deviation is declared in the headers of exp/L4b.sh (20:51:54) and exp/l4b_rollouts_pool3.py (20:51:42), written before the GPU job started, and in the first line of run.log. Say "declared in the script headers before launch", and note that the backlog entry was not amended. The backlog's own caveat (5% budget near 50 calls on 1,319 prompts) is why the pool was enlarged, and the enlargement also raised the 5% refit count to 2 and the 10% call count to about 200, which favours the rule.
(5) Minor: the file list omits groups.parquet, rollouts.parquet, feats.parquet, cmd.sh, wrap.sh and done.flag in runs/L4b/. No numeric error found.

Corrected verdict: Claim 2 at 5% (config A, floor 40): KILL by the rule, unchanged (+0.00 pp), robust to splits, cuts, bootstrap and to dropping the deviation; carry it as "no gain where plain fixed already hits 92% at 5%", not as a measured null on a comparable pool. Claim 2 at 10% (config B, floor 40): PASS by the rule on the 2,792-prompt pool as run (+5.01 pp, unchanged), but deviation-dependent: on the registered test-only rows a stand-in replay gives +0.58 pp (below +2.0), and at 1,300-prompt scale B averages +2.6 to +4.0 pp across pools, so carry B at 10% as "PASS at 2,800 to 3,000 prompts, marginal at 1,300, 88 of 100 pool-level bootstrap resamples above the bar". Claims 2 (as worded) and 3 readings (not_supported) unchanged. L5a and L5b remain sensible; L5a and L5b should be run at the full pool size, and any repeat on the test split alone should be pre-registered as a different, smaller-scale test.

## L5a 2026-10-18 pass

**Hypothesis:** The 10% gain of config B (stratum agreement x n_distinct x any_truncated, k0 50) comes from a stratum ranking fixed once from labels, not from the online refit. A table fitted once on the L1B pool (p0) with no refit should reproduce the gain on the L2b and L4b pools.

**What was run:** avrl/exp/l5a_frozen_table.py, a copy of l4b_replay.py (p0 and L4b scripts untouched). CPU only, /homes/rdubash/active-audit/.venv/bin/python -I. No new GPU job. Pools: existing feats for p0 (3,000 prompts, 436 proxy errors, used only for the fit), L2b (3,000 prompts) and L4b (2,792 prompts). Label: proxy_error (majority vote vs gold, GSM8K). Table: 36 strata from p0, posterior (errors + 50 x (1 - agreement)) / (count + 50), frozen, never refit. Eval: split seeds 0 to 7 (sims of 2,196 to 2,369 docs on L2b, 1,989 to 2,162 on L4b, 75 of 100 cell buckets), 8 tie-break seeds per split, budget int(0.10 x n_sim) distinct docs. Comparator: plain fixed threshold on the same split and tie-break seed. Gates before the verdict: plain fixed at 10% reproduces runs/L2b/result.json and runs/L4b/result.json with max abs diff 0.000000 pp (tol 0.01) and matching calls; spend gate 384 checks, 0 failures.

**Numbers** (hit rate in %, gap in pp against plain fixed at 10%, mean over split seeds 0 to 7, sd with ddof 1 over the 8 split-level gaps):

| pool | role | fixed 10% (%) | frozen B 10% (%) | frozen minus fixed (pp) | sd (pp) | splits above +2.0 | refitted B floor 40 (pp) | refitted B floor 10 (pp) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| L2b | verdict | 68.52 | 74.17 | +5.65 | 1.10 | 8 of 8 | +5.02 (runs/L3a/fresh) | +5.13 |
| L4b | verdict | 77.60 | 85.98 | +8.38 | 1.09 | 8 of 8 | +5.01 | +5.67 |
| p0 | in-sample reference only, outside the verdict | 70.46 | 76.19 | +5.74 | 0.87 | 8 of 8 | +4.64 (runs/L3a/l1b_pool) | not run |

Per split (pp): L2b +5.08, +7.02, +4.84, +7.40, +5.72, +6.01, +4.73, +4.43. L4b +7.87, +7.16, +8.02, +10.45, +7.51, +8.96, +7.77, +9.26. Frozen minus refitted B at floor 40: +0.63 on L2b, +3.37 on L4b.

**Verdict against the pre-registered rule:** pass threshold is a mean above +2.0 pp on both eval pools. L2b +5.65 and L4b +8.38 both clear it, with every split above +2.0 on both pools (lowest +4.43 on L2b, +7.16 on L4b). Kill (at or below +2.0 on either pool) is not triggered. Overall PASS.

**Independent check (lead, before logging):** recomputed the p0 table with plain pandas, not through the L1b module. 36 strata, 3,000 prompts, 436 errors, and every count, error count and posterior matches runs/L5a/result.json (max abs diff 0.0). 100% of L2b and L4b docs fall in table strata. Zero prompt-text overlap between p0 and L2b, and between p0 and L4b. Recomputed the L2b and L4b means and sds from the per-split gaps: match. Frozen score is attached per row before build_sim, and the Sim class takes y and score from the same frame, so they stay aligned.

**What it changes:**
- The 10% gain does not need the online refit. A ranking fixed from p0 labels before the eval pools were touched gives the same or a larger gain on both pools. The adaptive direction is therefore not what produces config B's 10% gain.
- Claim 2 as worded ("any fixed rule") must be restated. The frozen table is a fixed rule and it beats the learned refit on both pools (+0.63 on L2b, +3.37 on L4b). The comparison is label-confounded: the frozen table used about 3,000 labels, the refit about 200. Until the 200-label control (L6b below) is run, learned against fixed cannot be compared on equal labels.
- Claim 2 as worded: not_supported. Claim 2 restated against plain fixed at 10%, with the label caveat, is what the data support.
- Claim 3: the table uses only rollout-side strata (agreement, n_distinct, any_truncated) and no prompt feature. The 10% gain reproduces without prompt-space structure, so this run gives no support to "the edge comes from structure in prompt space". not_supported (unchanged).

**Caveats:**
- Pool composition. L4b is 2,792 prompts, of which 1,473 are remaining train rows (53%). The L4b critic found refit B at +0.58 on the 1,319 test rows alone. The frozen table's +8.38 is on the full pool, so the same question applies, and it has not been tested on the test rows. This is the largest open risk to the L5a L4b result.
- The L2b floor-40 reference is not in runs/L2b/result.json (that file holds floor 10 only). It comes from runs/L3a/fresh/result.json, which replays the same L2b feats file. The spec named the L2b result.json file, so this is a source deviation, not a number change.
- Label budget: about 3,000 frozen labels against about 200 for the refit. This isolates ranking fixedness, not label budget.
- L4b error rate is 0.214 against 0.145 on p0, so gaps are not directly comparable across pools.

**Follow-ups:**
1. L6a, frozen table on the L4b composition split (CPU, about 10 min). Apply the unchanged p0 table to the 1,319 test rows and the 1,473 remaining train rows of L4b separately, plus 64 random 1,319-prompt halves of L4b and L2b, at 10%, same gates. Pass: frozen gap above +2.0 on the test rows. Kill: at or below +2.0. Expected information value high: it decides whether the L5a PASS on L4b survives the composition problem the L4b critic raised.
2. L6b, label-budget control (CPU, about 15 min). Fit the same frozen table on 20 random 200-prompt subsets of p0 (k0 50), freeze each, and evaluate on L2b and L4b at 10%. Pass: mean gap above +2.0 on both pools at 200 labels. Expected information value high for claim 2: it puts the frozen table and the refit on the same label budget. If it holds near +5 pp, the 10% gain is a fixed ranking that needs little labelling. If it falls near zero, the 3,000-label table carries the gain.
3. L6c, frozen 5% control for config A (CPU, about 10 min). Freeze config A's table (agreement x n_distinct, k0 2) from p0 and apply it at 5% on L2b and L4b. Pass: above +2.0 on both pools. Kill: at or below +2.0 on either. Expected information value medium-high: refit A gave 0.00 on L4b at 5%. If the frozen A table gains there, the 5% failure is refit cadence, not the ranking.

Critic note (L5a, 2026-10-18). Checked the L5a entry against runs/L5a/result.json, table.md, run.log, exit_code, done.flag, exp/l5a_frozen_table.py, exp/L5a.sh, exp/l5a_verify.py, the L5a backlog section in lit/avrl/EXPERIMENT_BACKLOG.md, the reference results (runs/L2b/result.json, runs/L3a/fresh/result.json, runs/L3a/l1b_pool/result.json, runs/L4b/result.json), and the rollouts.parquet, groups.parquet and feats.parquet of p0, L2b and L4b. Independent checks are exp/l5a_critic_indep.py and exp/l5a_critic_composition.py (own code, no import of l1b_tuned_threshold or l5a_frozen_table), logs in runs/L5a_critic/ (indep.log, composition.log).

What checks out. Every number in the entry matches the raw files: per-split gaps (L2b +5.08, +7.02, +4.84, +7.40, +5.72, +6.01, +4.73, +4.43; L4b +7.87, +7.16, +8.02, +10.45, +7.51, +8.96, +7.77, +9.26), means and sds (L2b +5.65 / 1.10, L4b +8.38 / 1.09, p0 +5.74 / 0.87), fixed and frozen hit rates (68.52 / 74.17, 77.60 / 85.98, 70.46 / 76.19), the refit reference columns (floor 40: +5.02, +5.01, +4.64; floor 10: +5.13 and +5.67), the gates (reproduction diff 1.4e-14 on L2b and 0 on L4b, calls match, spend gate 384 checks and 0 failures) and the differences +0.63 and +3.37. The run is clean: exit code 0, run.log has no warning or traceback, nothing truncated. The verdict follows from the pre-registered rule: both pool means are above +2.0 (L2b +5.65, L4b +8.38), so PASS. Own reimplementation: agreement, n_distinct, any_truncated, majority and proxy_error rebuilt from rollouts.parquet equal feats.parquet and groups.parquet on all three pools with 0 differences (p0 436 errors, L2b 460, L4b 597; the majority tie rule is lexicographic, 251, 255 and 317 prompts with a tied top count, not gold-aware); the p0 frozen table refit with plain pandas equals result.json (36 strata, 436 errors in 3,000 prompts, max abs posterior diff 0.0); zero question overlap p0-L2b, p0-L4b and L2b-L4b, no duplicate questions inside a pool; 35 eval strata on each of L2b and L4b, none missing from the table. Plain fixed and the frozen ranking replayed with my own code on splits 0 to 7 x 8 tie seeds reproduce every per-split gap, fixed hit rate and frozen hit rate on all three pools (max diff 0.0), with every policy taking exactly int(0.10 x n_sim) distinct docs (0 failures). No leakage: the score is a function of agreement, n_distinct and any_truncated looked up in a table fitted on p0 labels only, and gold reaches only the p0 fit and the hit counting. Budget accounting is equal at eval time (same call count per split for fixed and frozen).

Corrections (rule verdict unchanged; wording and strength changed).
(1) "It beats the learned refit on both pools" holds on L4b only. On L2b the frozen table and refit B are level: +0.63 on the canonical splits, +0.54 over 64 random 75-of-100 bucket cuts (frozen above refit in 44 of 64, 95% interval -0.84 to +1.95), and +0.22 in a paired bucket bootstrap (100 resamples, 2 tie seeds, 95% interval -2.05 to +3.33, frozen above refit in 52 of 100). On L4b the difference is real: +3.39 over 64 cuts (64 of 64), +2.91 in the paired bootstrap (95% interval +0.32 to +6.53, 98 of 100). Write L2b as "ties the refit" and L4b as "exceeds the refit by about +3 pp".
(2) The open composition risk is partly answered, by a stand-in replay (same L4b pid split as the L4b critic, pid below 1319 are test rows, buckets from the joint cell100 clustering, p0 table unchanged). Test rows only (1,319 prompts, 26.2% proxy error): fixed 84.50, frozen 87.90, gap +3.39 (sd 2.21, splits +4.90, +3.62, -0.39, +1.39, +2.78, +5.93, +2.97, +5.94, 6 of 8 above +2.0); over 64 random bucket cuts +3.21 (sd 1.89, 48 of 64 above +2.0, min -1.64) against refit B +0.16 (2 of 64 above +2.0). Remaining train rows only (1,473 prompts, 17.0% error): fixed 69.35, frozen 82.09, gap +12.74 (8 of 8; 64 cuts +12.94). So the frozen table survives the move to the registered test rows above the +2.0 bar, where refit B did not, but with a thin margin (about +1.2 to +1.4 pp over the bar) and the +8.38 headline is inflated by the remaining-train rows. Random 1,319-prompt halves (6 each): L4b +2.79, +8.83, +7.30, +8.93, +6.79, +6.08 (mean +6.79, 6 of 6 above +2.0); L2b +0.77, +6.74, +4.10, +4.74, +5.20, +10.74 (mean +5.38, 5 of 6). This is a stand-in, not the real L6a run; L6a should still be run, and the expected reading is a narrow pass.
(3) "8 of 8 splits above +2.0" overstates independence: the eight splits overlap inside one pool. Bucket bootstrap of the frozen gap (100 resamples of 75 buckets with replacement, 3 tie seeds, duplicated buckets inflate spread): L2b mean +5.87, sd 3.22, 95% interval -0.15 to +12.58, 90 of 100 above +2.0; L4b mean +8.60, sd 2.68, 95% interval +4.71 to +14.43, 99 of 100 above +2.0. So the L4b pass is firm at pool level and the L2b pass is firm in direction (90 of 100), about as firm as the refit B reading on that pool (187 of 200 in the L3a critic).
(4) The interpretation is stated more strongly than the label confound allows. "The adaptive direction is therefore not what produces config B's 10% gain" is the pre-registered reading of the PASS, but the frozen table used 3,000 labels (15 times the refit's roughly 200 in-budget labels) while the refit started from the prior alone and reached the same gain on L2b from its own calls. What the data support is "a fixed, label-derived ranking from 3,000 labels matches (L2b) or exceeds (L4b) the 200-label refit", not "adaptivity adds nothing at equal labels"; L6b is the experiment that separates the two, and until it runs, claim 2 should be restated against label-derived fixed rules with the label count attached.
(5) Minor: the entry says p0 was "used only for the fit", but p0 is also replayed in-sample as a reference row (+5.74, outside the verdict), and the 384 spend checks include those p0 replays (128 per pool). "Before the eval pools were touched" is true for the table fit but the L2b and L4b rollouts and earlier replays already existed; the table and the config B hyperparameters (k0 50, three-feature stratum) were not tuned on them. No numeric error found.

Corrected verdict: PASS by the rule, unchanged (frozen minus fixed at 10%: L2b +5.65, L4b +8.38, both above +2.0, reproduced exactly). Carry it as: a fixed ranking fitted once on 3,000 p0 labels gains +5.65 pp on L2b (level with refit B at +5.02) and +8.38 pp on L4b (about +3 pp above refit B at +5.01), firm at pool level on L4b (99 of 100 bootstrap resamples above +2.0) and fairly firm on L2b (90 of 100); on the L4b test rows alone the stand-in gives +3.2 to +3.4 pp, above the bar but thin, so the headline +8.38 is partly pool composition.
