- Operating point. The registered rows sit at a fixed hit rate (84.5%) that no L2b cut reaches (cut range 64.5% to 74.3%). They have 15.5 points of headroom against 31.5 on L2b, so the gain is mechanically compressed there. The headroom-normalized ratio is inside the cut band, but it was computed after the run and is not a result (see the follow-ups).
- The 48 cuts overlap (about 580 shared rows per pair), so p5 and p95 describe this design, not a calibrated interval.
- Bootstrap draws keep duplicates (about 830 distinct rows per draw). The spend check counts row positions, so duplicates count as distinct. The bootstrap sd is a row-level approximation and was not validated.
- Only the fixed and frozen arms are scored, as registered. The L13a refit arms do not enter frozen minus fixed.

What it changes about the thesis claims:
- The registered test-row gain is not a representative comparator at this sample size. It sits in the bottom 2% of 1,319-prompt cuts of L2b. The check does not establish whether the cause is the question source, the rollout run, or headroom.
- The sign of the learned-arm gain is not in doubt on these pools. Frozen beats plain fixed on all 48 L2b cuts (minimum +1.16 pp), on the L2b and L4b full pools (+4.72 and +5.12 pp), and on the remaining L4b rows (+8.58 pp). What fails is the size on the registered rows (+1.22 pp, with a bootstrap interval that includes zero).
- Claim 2 (a learned allocator admits fewer corrupted rewards than any fixed rule at equal budget): stays not_supported. "Not_supported" here means the registered comparator does not carry the claim, not that the allocator is refuted. Only one fixed rule (agreement ascending, then n_distinct descending) has been tested, so "any fixed rule" is untested in scope.
- Claim 3 (the edge comes from structure in prompt space): stays not_supported. L16a has no prompt-space variable.
- L16b in the backlog still carries the 9.4 pp reference. The L15b critic found that its no-tercile reference is 3.4 pp on L16b's own metric, so L16b needs its thresholds re-registered before it runs. Not edited here.

Follow-ups (iteration 17, appended to lit/avrl/EXPERIMENT_BACKLOG.md under "Proposed by the loop, iteration 17 (after L16a)"):
1. L17a, run-matched split null (CPU, existing feats). Compare the registered test rows with the remaining L4b rows, which share one rollout run. D = gain(test) minus gain(remaining), from 200 draws of 1,000 rows without replacement from each group. Pass (the split is not special within the run): 0 lies between the 5th and 95th percentiles of D. Kill: the 95th percentile of D is below 0. Expected information value high: it is the only check that holds the rollout run fixed, so it decides whether the registered-row shortfall is a split effect at all.
2. L17b, headroom-normalized comparator (CPU, existing runs/L16a numbers, no new compute). Ratio = gain divided by (100 minus the fixed hit rate), registered rows against the 48 L2b cuts. Pass: the test ratio lies between the 5th and 95th percentiles of the cut ratios. Kill: below the 5th percentile. Expected information value medium: if it passes, the registered shortfall is mostly headroom and claim 2 should be reported on that scale. It has to be registered as a new spec before it is read as a result.
3. L17c, second and third fixed rules (CPU, existing feats). Claim 2 says "any fixed rule", but only one has been tested. Add n_distinct descending alone and mean_tokens descending as fixed rules at 10%, and score frozen against each on the L2b full pool and the registered rows. Pass: frozen minus each rule is above +1.0 pp on the L2b full pool. Kill: any rule's gap is at or below 0 on the L2b full pool. Expected information value high for the scope of claim 2.

Critic note (L16a, 2026-10-19). Checked the L16a entry against runs/L16a/result.json, gate.json, all 48 cuts/cut_*.json and 200 boot/boot_*.json, every log, critic/replay.json, exp/l16a_source_split_null.py, exp/L16a.sh, exp/l16a_critic_indep.py, the L13a helpers, runs/L13a/{L4b_pid_le1318,L4b_pid_gt1318,L4b,L2b}/result.json, the p0, L2b and L4b feats.parquet files, the L2b and L4b rollout scripts and the L16a registration in lit/avrl/EXPERIMENT_BACKLOG.md. I did not rerun the 48 cuts or the 200 draws (the stored replay imports no experiment module and matches to 5.3e-15 pp); I re-derived the cut and bootstrap row positions from RandomState(100 + c) and RandomState(16) and they equal the stored positions. Numbers: the cut mean, sd, p5, p95, min, median, max (+4.351, 1.997, +1.888, +8.246, +1.162, +4.009, +9.828), the 1 of 48 count, the bootstrap p5, p50, p95 and sd (-1.509, +1.008, +3.751, 1.569), the hit rates (84.5 / 85.7, 69.6 / 74.0, 68.5 / 73.2), the fixed-rate range 64.5 to 74.3, the proxy error rates (26.2, 17.0, 15.3), the remaining-row +8.58 and L4b full-pool +5.12, the headroom ratios (0.079, 0.058, 0.288, 7 of 48 lower), the 580 shared rows per pair (measured 580.2), the 830 distinct rows per draw (measured 833.9), the 248 frames x 2,560 = 634,880 spend checks, and the rollout accuracies 63.6% and 58.2% all match the raw files. Rule check: +1.2223 is below the p5 of +1.8879, so KILL follows from the registered rule. It does not depend on the percentile definition: p5 is 1.57 (Weibull), 1.70, 1.75, 1.79 (lower) or 2.06 (higher), all above +1.22, and +1.22 lies between the minimum (1.16) and the second smallest cut (1.39). The above-p95 branch does not apply. No leakage: the frozen score is the p0-fitted stratum posterior (agreement x n_distinct x any_truncated), the tie key and sort use agreement and n_distinct only, cell100 is KMeans on question embeddings, gold enters only through p0 labels (fit) and the hit count (scoring), and question overlap of L2b, the test rows and the remaining rows with p0 and with each other is 0 (rechecked). Budget: fixed_pick and frozen_pick share the same sim, the same int(0.10 x n_sim) budget and the same per-seed tie key, and both are asserted to hold exactly budget distinct rows; 0 failures. Logs: no warning, error or traceback text in any log, gate.exit is 0, each cut batch has 8 lines and each boot batch 50. Corrections, none of which changes the verdict: (1) "n_sim about 950 to 1,040" is narrower than the files: per-split n_sim runs 910 to 1,069 across the 48 cuts and 957 to 1,017 on the registered rows. (2) "both L13a references reproduce (diffs 0.0 and 1.4e-14 pp)": the two gain references differ by 0.0 each; the 1.4e-14 diffs are on the test-row frozen hit rate and the L2b fixed hit rate. (3) "It sits in the bottom 2%" reads as a p-value of 0.02, which 48 cuts cannot resolve. One cut is at or below +1.22; counting the test value as a 49th draw, the one-sided empirical tail is 2 of 49 = 0.041. The p5 line itself is soft: resampling the 48 cut means with replacement (5,000 resamples, computed in the shell from the stored files) gives a p5 with sd 0.36 pp (5th to 95th range 1.24 to 2.44), so the 0.67 pp margin is about 1.8 of those sd, and 2.1% of resamples put p5 at or below +1.22. The verdict also rests on averaging the 20 fit tables: resampling the tables (2,000 resamples, same tables for test and cuts) keeps KILL in 95.5%, but taken one table at a time the test value is below that table's own cut p5 in only 8 of 20 tables, and the test-row table values run from -1.55 to +3.78 (sd 1.90, se of the 20-table mean about 0.42). (4) The overlap caveat needs its direction. Each cut holds 44% of the 3,000-row pool, so the cut spread (sd 2.00) is narrower than the spread of independent 1,319-row draws. Under an additive finite-population argument (a heuristic, not tested here) independent draws would have sd about 2.00 / sqrt(1 - 1319 / 3000) = 2.67, which moves the test z from -1.57 to -1.17; the cut means are also right-skewed (skew +0.96), so normal tails are only indicative. So the registered p5 is probably tighter than a calibrated 5th percentile, which makes the kill rule easier to trigger, not harder. (5) Two sentences overreach: "It sits in the bottom 2% of 1,319-prompt cuts" and "The registered test-row gain is not a representative comparator at this sample size". Supported reading: the test-row gain is below 47 of 48 cuts and fails the registered pass band, with a one-sided tail of about 0.04 to 0.1 depending on calibration, and its own bootstrap interval (-1.51 to +3.75, 23.5% of the 200 draws at or below zero) shows its sampling noise is of the same size as the kill margin. With rollout run, question source and headroom confounded, L16a does not show that the test split lowers the gain. Corrected verdict: KILL, unchanged and on the registered rule alone (+1.22 pp below the 48-cut p5 of +1.89 pp), read as a marginal kill. Claims 2 and 3 stay not_supported. L17a, which holds the rollout run fixed, is the discriminating next check, and L17b should be registered with the fragility in (3) and (4) in view.

## L9b 2026-10-19 registered (rule fixed before the run, no result yet)

Hypothesis: with the greedy_prior logistic scorer fitted on all 3,000 p0 labels, the greedy learned arm beats the 3,000-label frozen stratum table (L5a) at a 10% budget by more than +1.0 pp on both L2b and L4b. Null: it does not. Under the null, the L7a 200-label kill reflects a ceiling in the six-feature logistic family rather than label starvation.

Registered rule: per pool, per split seed 0 to 7, the mean over 8 tie-break seeds of (greedy_prior hit rate minus the frozen 3,000-label table hit rate) at 10%, in pp. The pool value is the mean over the 8 split gaps, with sd (ddof 1) over splits. PASS per pool if the mean is above +1.0 pp; KILL if at or below +1.0 pp on either pool. Overall PASS needs both pools. Context: table minus plain fixed must reproduce L5a (+5.65 L2b, +8.38 L4b).

Design: avrl/exp/l9b_learned_3000_labels.py (a copy of the L7a flow, L7a unchanged) with one fit on all 3,000 p0 rows for the greedy initial scorer and every refit (refits add verified eval labels, as in L7a). The comparator is the L5a table, fit once on all 3,000 p0 rows with k0 50 and never refit. Gates before any verdict: plain fixed reproduces runs/L2b (B) and runs/L4b (B40) per split, table minus fixed reproduces runs/L5a per split, spend gates, zero eval question overlap with p0. The L6b per-subset gate does not apply (no subsets) and is recorded as not applicable. Independent check: avrl/exp/l9b_check.py recomputes all split gaps from the saved hit counts (runs/L9b/result.json raw_hits) and compares them with the experiment's own numbers.

Interpretation fixed in advance: a pass means the learned arm can match the table at 3,000 equal labels, which gives claim 2 a live learned arm to test further. It is not a claim pass alone. A kill at both 200 labels (L7a) and 3,000 labels points to a ceiling in the six-feature logistic family. A pass at 3,000 with the L7a kill at 200 points to scorer under-fitting from a small sample. Only one learned arm is tested here.

## L9b 2026-10-19 kill (both pools: greedy_prior trails the 3,000-label table by 2.38 pp on L2b and 1.59 pp on L4b)

Result, as registered (rule above, unchanged). All gates pass: plain fixed reproduces runs/L2b (B) and runs/L4b (B40) per split (max abs diff 0.000000 pp); the 3,000-label table minus plain fixed reproduces runs/L5a per split (0.000000 pp), giving +5.65 on L2b and +8.38 on L4b; spend gates 256 and 128 checks with 0 failures; zero eval question text in p0. The L6b per-subset gate is not applicable (no subsets).

| pool | greedy_prior minus 3,000-label table, mean over split seeds 0 to 7 (pp) | sd over splits (pp) | splits above +1.0 | range over splits (pp) | 3,000-label table minus plain fixed (pp) | greedy_prior minus plain fixed (pp) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| L2b | -2.38 | 0.67 | 0 of 8 | -3.26 to -1.23 | +5.65 | +3.27 |
| L4b | -1.59 | 0.87 | 0 of 8 | -3.02 to -0.75 | +8.38 | +6.78 |

Verdict: KILL on both pools (both means at or below +1.0; overall KILL). All 16 split gaps are negative, so no split comes near the bar.

Reading against the L7a kill: at 200 labels the greedy arm trailed the frozen table by -2.06 pp (L2b) and -3.35 pp (L4b). At 3,000 labels the gap is -2.38 pp on L2b (not closed, slightly wider) and -1.59 pp on L4b (about half closed). More labels for the scorer do not close the gap on L2b and only partly close it on L4b. The reading fixed in advance was that a kill at both sizes points to a ceiling in the six-feature logistic family rather than label starvation. The data support that reading for the greedy logistic arm on this pair. Two limits: all 3,000 p0 rows are used, so this run cannot resample the p0 fit, and only one learned arm (logistic scorer, six cheap features) was tested.

Greedy minus plain fixed grows with labels: +2.66 to +3.27 pp on L2b and +1.77 to +6.78 pp on L4b. So the learned arm does gain over plain fixed at 3,000 labels, but it stays below the table on both pools.

Claims: no change. No learned arm has passed at equal labels on either pool, so claim 2 stays not_supported on this pair. Claim 3 reading unchanged.

Independent check: avrl/exp/l9b_check.py recomputes all 16 split gaps from the saved hit counts (runs/L9b/result.json, raw_hits), using numpy and json only. It matches the experiment's per-split gaps, pool means, sd values and verdicts to 0.000000 pp, and matches the L5a table-minus-fixed gaps per split. Log: runs/L9b/indep_check.log (check PASS).

## L9b 2026-10-19 kill (addendum: verification and follow-ups)

Verification. This step reread runs/L9b/result.json, table.md, run.log, indep_check.log and indep_check.json, and did not rely on the run report. It recomputed all 16 split gaps, both pool means and sds, the table minus plain fixed reference (+5.65 on L2b, +8.38 on L4b) and the 0 of 8 counts from raw_hits with stdlib Python (not avrl/exp/l9b_check.py). All match the stored values to 4 decimal places. Shape checks: budget equals int(0.10 x n_sim) on all 16 splits, 8 tie seeds per arm per split, every hit count between 0 and budget, and greedy tie-seed spread 0 on both pools. The 200-label context in the entry above matches runs/L7a/result.json (greedy minus table -2.06 on L2b and -3.35 on L4b; greedy minus plain fixed +2.66 on L2b and +1.77 on L4b). The run scripts contain no ssh or pip commands. The greedy scorer is one fit on all 3,000 p0 rows (fit_set passed through, no subset draw).

Caveat on "equal labels". The greedy arm's refits also use its own verified eval labels, up to the per-split budget (198 to 236 rows), on top of the 3,000 p0 labels. The frozen table uses only the 3,000 p0 labels. So the kill is against a learned arm that had more label information, not less. This asymmetry is the design registered in L7a, unchanged here.

Claims. Claim 2 (a learned allocator admits fewer corrupted rewards than any fixed rule at equal budget): not_supported, unchanged. No learned arm has passed at equal labels on either pool. Claim 3 (the edge comes from structure in prompt space): not_supported, unchanged. The table's keys are rollout-side. The greedy arm's six cheap features include one prompt-side scalar (log_qlen), which this run does not isolate, so it gives no prompt-space evidence either way.

On the reading. "A ceiling in the six-feature logistic family" fits the data but is not yet shown. The logistic scorer has no interaction terms, while the 3,000-label table is a full 36-cell cross of agreement x n_distinct x any_truncated. L18a tests that directly. L18b tests whether the gap sits in the scorer's ranking or in the online refit loop.

Follow-ups (iteration 18, registered in lit/avrl/EXPERIMENT_BACKLOG.md under "Proposed by the loop, iteration 18 (after L9b)"; none run yet):

1. L18a, stratum-indicator logistic scorer at 3,000 labels (CPU, existing feats). Pass: greedy minus the 3,000-label table above +1.0 pp on both pools. Kill: at or below +1.0 pp on either pool. Expected information value high: it tests the ceiling reading directly. A pass would register an interaction-aware learned family as a new arm, not rescue the six-feature arm.
2. L18b, one-shot logistic ranking with no online refit (CPU, existing feats). Pass (the refit loop is the cost): one-shot minus the table above +1.0 pp on both pools. Kill (the scorer's ranking is the cost): at or below +1.0 pp on either pool. Expected information value high: it splits the L9b gap between scorer and refit loop and picks L18a or a refit fix as the next step.
3. L18c, L9c at 3,000 labels on the registered test rows (pid <= 1318 of L4b, CPU, existing feats). Pass: above +1.0 pp. Kill: at or below +1.0 pp. Expected information value medium-low: every full-L4b split gap is already negative in L9b, so a kill is likely and mostly confirms L9b on those rows. L9c at 200 labels stays open.

Critic note (L9b, 2026-10-19). Checked the L9b entries (registration, kill, addendum) against runs/L9b/result.json (including raw_hits), table.md, run.log, exit_code, indep_check.log, indep_check.json, done.flag, exp/L9b.sh, exp/l9b_learned_3000_labels.py, exp/l9b_check.py, exp/l1b_tuned_threshold.py (build_sim, Sim), runs/L7a/result.json, runs/L5a/result.json and the p0, L2b and L4b feats.parquet files. Numbers: every figure in the entry matches the raw files. Per-split gaps, pool means and sds (-2.3809 / 0.6652 on L2b, -1.5926 / 0.8669 on L4b, ddof 1), 0 of 8 above +1.0, min and max (-3.26 / -1.23, -3.02 / -0.75), table minus plain fixed (+5.65, +8.38), greedy minus plain fixed (+3.27, +6.78), hit rates (68.52 / 74.17 / 71.79 and 77.60 / 85.98 / 84.39), gates (max abs diff 0.0, 256 and 128 spend checks, 0 failures, eval overlap 0 and 0), budgets (198 to 236 = int(0.10 x n_sim) on all 16 splits, n_sim 1,989 to 2,369), greedy tie-seed spread 0, the runtime of about 24 s (run.log created 01:17:19.02, last written 01:17:42.64), and the L7a context (-2.06 and -3.35 vs the 200-label table; greedy minus fixed +2.66 and +1.77). Rule check: both pool means are at or below +1.0, so KILL on both and overall KILL follows from the registered rule. Independent replay: I reimplemented the three arms in my own code (plain fixed, the 36-stratum table posterior with k0 50 and prior 1 - agreement, and greedy_prior with StandardScaler plus balanced LogisticRegression, refit every max(budget // 8, 40) picks on p0 plus verified eval labels), rebuilt each split with default_rng(split) on cell100 buckets (MIN 3, first quarter dropped), and compared 288 hit counts (16 splits x 8 fixed, 8 frozen and greedy tie seeds 0 and 5). All 288 equal raw_hits exactly, so the saved hit counts are verified, not only the arithmetic that l9b_check.py redoes. No leakage: the greedy features are agreement, n_distinct, mean_tokens, std_tokens, any_truncated and log_qlen (agreement is share of rollouts matching the majority answer, gold-free; pass_rate and majority_correct are not used), gold reaches only the p0 fit labels, the verified labels of docs already picked, and hit counting. Question overlap of L2b and L4b with p0 is 0 by exact text, by lowercased alphanumeric text and by 60-character prefix, with no duplicates inside any pool. Budget accounting is equal (same int(0.10 x n_sim) distinct docs per arm, same split and tie seed; asserted). Logs: no warning, error or traceback text in run.log or indep_check.log, both exit codes 0. No silent truncation or crash. Corrections, none of which changes the verdict: (1) "the 3,000-label table is a full 36-cell cross" is wrong as worded: the table holds 36 observed strata out of 128 possible combinations (8 agreement levels x 8 n_distinct values x 2 truncation flags), 9 of them with under 10 p0 prompts and the smallest with 1, and each eval pool occupies 35 of them. The substantive point (the table has interactions, the logistic has none) stands. (2) The sd over splits (0.67, 0.87) and the line "all 16 split gaps are negative, so no split comes near the bar" understate the uncertainty. The 8 splits are group shuffles of one pool that keep 75% of the same buckets, and all share one p0 fit, so they are far from 16 independent confirmations; they carry neither eval-row sampling noise nor p0 resampling noise. As a diagnostic (computed in the shell, not registered, nothing written), I drew 60 random half-pools per pool (RandomState(900 + d), one split per draw, p0 fit of 3,000, greedy against the table averaged over tie seeds 0 to 3, 10% budget). The gap has mean -2.65, sd 1.79, range -6.54 to +1.13 on L2b (2 of 60 above +1.0) and mean -1.94, sd 2.48, range -7.28 to +3.50 on L4b (7 of 60 above +1.0, 11.7%). That is 2 to 3 times the split sd. The regime differs from the registered one (half the rows, budgets near 110), so these are indicative only. Reading: the kill is clear on L2b and holds with a smaller margin on L4b (-1.59 against a bar of +1.0 is about one pool-resample sd of 2.5). (3) "More labels for the scorer do not close the gap on L2b" and "slightly wider" overreach. The L2b change from -2.06 to -2.38 is 0.32 pp. In the same half-pool diagnostic the 200-to-3,000 closure (gap at 3,000 labels minus gap at 200-label subsets RandomState(100 + i), i 0 to 4, same table fit on the same 200 rows) is +1.06 pp with sd 1.67 on L2b (positive in 72% of draws) and +1.98 pp with sd 2.11 on L4b (positive in 80%), so -0.32 on L2b is inside the noise and the supported statement is "no reliable closure seen on L2b", not "wider". The L4b "about half closed" (-3.35 to -1.59) is directionally consistent with the diagnostic but is not a measured effect size. (4) "The data support that reading" (ceiling in the six-feature logistic family) is too strong. The data are consistent with it: both pools stay below the table at 3,000 labels, the learned arm gains +3.27 and +6.78 over plain fixed, and no tested configuration clears the bar. The reading is a hypothesis for L18a to test, not a result of L9b. (5) A caution on the L18b rating of "high" information value: the greedy refits add at most 236 verified eval labels to 3,000 p0 labels (at most 7.3% of the fit data), so greedy_prior here is already close to a one-shot scorer and L18b will likely land near L9b unless the in-pool labels matter far more than their count. Its discriminating power is probably lower than rated; judge it against that prior before spending effort. Corrected verdict: KILL on both pools, unchanged, on the registered rule alone (L2b -2.38, L4b -1.59 against +1.0). Read as a clear kill on L2b and a kill with a modest margin on L4b once pool-sampling noise is counted. Claim 2 stays not_supported on this pair for the six-feature logistic arm at 3,000 p0 labels; the ceiling reading stays a hypothesis; claim 3 unchanged.

## L18a 2026-10-19 kill (both pools: greedy_strata trails the 3,000-label table by 1.89 pp on L2b and 2.39 pp on L4b)

Registration (rule as in the L9b addendum follow-ups, not changed before the run): the six CHEAP features plus one 0/1 indicator per p0 stratum cell (36 cells, agreement x n_distinct x any_truncated). Every L2b and L4b stratum is observed in p0, so the all-zero block was never used (0 unseen eval rows on both pools). Scorer unchanged: StandardScaler plus LogisticRegression(class_weight balanced, max_iter 1000), fitted on all 3,000 p0 rows, refit every max(budget // 8, 40) = 40 verified picks on p0 plus verified eval labels. Comparator: the L5a 3,000-label frozen table, fitted once with k0 50 and prior 1 - agreement, never refit. Splits 0 to 7, 8 tie-break seeds, budget int(0.10 x n_sim). Pass: pool mean above +1.0 pp on both pools. Kill: at or below +1.0 pp on either pool.

Result (avrl/runs/L18a/result.json, table.md). Metric is the mean over splits of the 8-tie-seed mean of greedy_strata minus the frozen table, in pp.
- L2b: mean -1.89 pp, sd over the 8 split gaps 0.38, min -2.39, max -1.23, 0 of 8 splits above +1.0. Verdict KILL.
- L4b: mean -2.39 pp, sd 0.62, min -3.47, max -1.74, 0 of 8 splits above +1.0. Verdict KILL.
- Context: table minus plain fixed +5.65 (L2b) and +8.38 (L4b), matching L5a. Greedy minus plain fixed +3.77 (L2b) and +5.98 (L4b). Mean hit rates (%) fixed 68.52 / 77.60, table 74.17 / 85.98, greedy_strata 72.29 / 83.59 (L2b / L4b).
- Against L9b (six features, greedy_prior): L2b moved from -2.38 to -1.89 (+0.49 pp) and L4b from -1.59 to -2.39 (-0.80 pp). The two moves have opposite signs, so the stratum indicators do not close the gap on either pool.
- Sample sizes: n_sim 2,196 to 2,369 on L2b (budgets 219 to 236) and 1,989 to 2,162 on L4b (budgets 198 to 216), 8 splits per pool. Tie-break spread 0 hits on both pools, so the tie seed made no difference.

Gates (all PASS, run.log): plain fixed reproduces runs/L2b (B) and runs/L4b (B40) per split, max abs diff 0.000000 pp (tol 0.01), calls match. Table minus plain fixed reproduces runs/L5a per split, max abs diff 0.000000 pp (tol 1e-6). Spend: 256 frozen and fixed checks and 128 greedy checks, 0 failures (every pick count equals budget with distinct docs). Eval question overlap with p0: 0 on both pools.

Independent check: avrl/exp/l18a_check.py (numpy and json only, the L9b checker with the names changed, imports nothing from the experiment) recomputes all 16 split gaps, pool means, sd values, counts and the verdict from result.json raw_hits. It matches result.json to 0.00e+00 pp and reproduces the L5a comparator per split. Log: runs/L18a/indep_check.log (PASS, exit 0).

Reading. The registered rule kills the arm on both pools. The missing-interaction reading (the L9b ceiling is a missing stratum interaction in the logistic family) is not supported on this pair: giving the logistic scorer one indicator per table cell still leaves it 1.9 and 2.4 pp below the table, and the L9b-to-L18a moves are inside the pool-resample noise (the L9b half-pool diagnostic put the L4b sd near 2.5 pp). No direction is claimed for the change. The kill applies to this estimator only: a balanced, L2-regularized (C = 1) logistic on standardized features, ranked by predicted probability. It is not the table's shrunken cell posterior ranking, so it does not rule out other learned scorers that rank by the cell posterior. Claim 2 stays not_supported on this pair for learned arms at 3,000 p0 labels, now including the stratum-indicator logistic arm. The frozen table stays the deliverable. The pass path that L18a was meant to open is closed for this scorer family.

## L18a 2026-10-19 kill (addendum: recheck, table, claim readings, follow-ups)

Verification. This step reread runs/L18a/result.json, table.md, run.log, indep_check.json and indep_check.log, and checked exit_code and indep_check_exit_code (both 0). It recomputed the 16 split gaps, both pool means and sds, the 0 of 8 counts, and the table minus plain fixed reference from raw_hits with a separate stdlib script (not avrl/exp/l18a_check.py). All match the stored values. Shape checks pass: budget equals int(0.10 x n_sim) on every split, 8 tie seeds per arm, every hit count between 0 and budget, greedy tie-seed spread 0 on both pools. The scorer reads gold only through the p0 fit labels and the verified eval labels. Its features are the six CHEAP columns plus 36 p0-cell indicators, with no pass_rate or majority_correct column. The runbook avrl/exp/L18a.sh runs locally on CPU only (no gpu_run.sh, no ssh, no pip).

| pool | greedy_strata minus 3,000-label table (pp) | sd over splits (pp) | splits above +1.0 | greedy minus plain fixed (pp) | table minus plain fixed (pp) | hit rate fixed / table / greedy (%) | verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| L2b | -1.89 | 0.38 | 0 of 8 | +3.77 | +5.65 | 68.52 / 74.17 / 72.29 | kill |
| L4b | -2.39 | 0.62 | 0 of 8 | +5.98 | +8.38 | 77.60 / 85.98 / 83.59 | kill |

Baselines: the L9b greedy_prior minus table gap was -2.38 (L2b) and -1.59 (L4b). The stratum indicators moved it +0.49 on L2b and -0.80 on L4b, so the change has no consistent sign.

Verdict against the registered rule. Pass needs both pool means above +1.0 pp. Neither pool is (-1.89 and -2.39). The kill rule (at or below +1.0 pp on either pool) holds on both. Verdict: kill.

What it changes. The stratum-interaction reading of the L9b ceiling is rejected for this estimator (balanced, C = 1 logistic, ranked by predicted probability). The learned arm still beats plain fixed by +3.77 and +5.98 pp, so the logistic family is not useless, but it does not reach the frozen table on either pool. Scorers that rank by the cell posterior were not tested here.

Claims. Claim 2 (a learned allocator admits fewer corrupted rewards than any fixed rule at equal budget): not_supported, unchanged. The learned arm beats plain fixed but trails the 3,000-label frozen table, the registered comparator, on both pools. Only one fixed rule has been tested. Claim 3 (the edge comes from structure in prompt space): not_supported, unchanged. All 36 stratum indicators are rollout-side (agreement, n_distinct, any_truncated), so this run gives no prompt-space evidence. log_qlen is the one prompt-side column among the six CHEAP features, and this run does not isolate it.

Follow-ups (filed under iteration 19 in lit/avrl/EXPERIMENT_BACKLOG.md, none run):
1. L19a, cell-posterior greedy with online updates (CPU, existing feats). Rank docs by the L5a shrunken cell posterior (k0 50, prior 1 minus agreement), recomputed after each refit block from p0 rows plus verified eval labels. Pass: above +1.0 pp on both pools against the frozen table. Kill: at or below +1.0 pp on either. Information value medium-high: it closes the gap this entry leaves open, and it separates the table's ranking from the logistic's probability ranking.
2. L19b, stratum-cell decomposition of the one-shot gap (CPU, existing feats, no new labels). Split the one-shot logistic minus frozen table hit difference by the 36 p0 cells. Metric: the share of that difference that falls in cells with fewer than 10 p0 prompts (count them from the p0 fit set). Pass (the table's edge is structural): under 30 percent. Kill (the edge is a sparse-cell effect): at least 70 percent. Between: inconclusive. Information value high for how the frozen table's edge is reported.
3. L19c, GSM8K rollout replicate on the 1,319 registered test questions (GPU, one gpu_run.sh job, about 8 min of generation by the p0 throughput estimate, same model and prompt as the p0 rollouts). Pass: the table minus plain fixed gain on the test rows is above +1.0 pp and inside the 5th to 95th percentile of the 48 L16a train cuts. Kill: at or below +1.0 pp, or below the 5th percentile. Information value high: it separates the rollout run from the question source, the L16a confound (test-row gain +1.22 pp against +8.58 pp for the remaining rows).

The L18b and L18c items from the L9b addendum stay open. L18b's information value is lower than that addendum rated it, because the refits add at most 236 verified labels to 3,000.

Critic note (L18a, 2026-10-19). Checked the L18a entries (registration, kill, addendum) against runs/L18a/result.json (including raw_hits), table.md, run.log, exit_code, indep_check.log, indep_check.json, indep_check_exit_code, done.flag, exp/L18a.sh, exp/l18a_strata_logistic_3000.py (exp/L18a.py does not exist; the script is the l18a_ one), exp/l18a_check.py, exp/l1b_tuned_threshold.py (build_sim, Sim), the registration in lit/avrl/EXPERIMENT_BACKLOG.md, runs/L9b/result.json, runs/L5a/result.json, runs/L16a/result.json and the p0, L2b and L4b feats.parquet files. Numbers: every figure in the entry matches the raw files. Per-split gaps, pool means and sds (-1.8859 / 0.3804 on L2b, -2.3915 / 0.6207 on L4b, ddof 1), 0 of 8 above +1.0, min and max (-2.39 / -1.23, -3.47 / -1.74), table minus plain fixed (+5.65, +8.38), greedy minus plain fixed (+3.77, +5.98), hit rates (68.52 / 74.17 / 72.29 and 77.60 / 85.98 / 83.59), n_sim 2,196 to 2,369 and 1,989 to 2,162, budgets 219 to 236 and 198 to 216 (= int(0.10 x n_sim) on all 16 splits), the gates (max abs diff 0 and 1.4e-14, 256 and 128 spend checks, 0 failures, eval overlap 0 and 0, 0 unseen-stratum eval rows, 42 scorer columns), the runtime of 17 s (run.log 01:31:21.09 to 01:31:38.37, script saved 01:31:05, before the run), and the L9b context (-2.38 and -1.59, so the moves are +0.495 and -0.799 pp). Registration check: the backlog L18a block (scorer, comparator, splits, tie seeds, budget, metric, pass and kill thresholds) is the run that was executed. Rule check: both pool means are at or below +1.0, so KILL on both and overall KILL follow from the registered rule. Independent replay: I reimplemented the three arms in my own code (plain fixed, the 36-stratum table posterior with k0 50 and prior 1 - agreement, and greedy_strata with StandardScaler plus balanced LogisticRegression on the six CHEAP columns plus 36 cell indicators, refit every max(budget // 8, 40) = 40 picks on p0 plus verified eval labels), rebuilt each split with default_rng(split) on cell100 buckets (MIN 3, first quarter dropped), and compared 288 hit counts (16 splits x 8 fixed, 8 frozen, plus greedy at tie seeds 0 and 5). All 288 equal raw_hits exactly. This matters because l18a_check.py and the addendum's stdlib script only re-aggregate the saved hit counts; neither regenerates them. No leakage: the scorer columns are agreement (cnt.max() / G over the rollouts' normalized answers, gold-free), n_distinct, mean_tokens, std_tokens, any_truncated, log_qlen (log1p of question length) and 36 indicators of the (agreement, n_distinct, any_truncated) cell; pass_rate, majority_correct, gold and majority are not used; cell100 is a question-embedding KMeans (gold-free); gold reaches only the p0 fit labels, the verified labels of docs already picked, and hit counting. The six columns have no NaN in p0, L2b or L4b, so the fillna(0) never fires. Eval question overlap with p0 is 0 and no pool has a duplicated question. Budget accounting is equal (same int(0.10 x n_sim) distinct docs per arm, same split and tie seed; asserted); as in L9b, the greedy arm also gets up to 236 verified eval labels through its refits that the table does not, so the kill is against an arm with more label information. Logs: no warning, error or traceback text in run.log or indep_check.log, both exit codes 0, so no silent truncation or crash. Corrections, none of which changes the verdict: (1) "Tie-break spread 0 hits on both pools, so the tie seed made no difference" is true only for greedy_strata. The fixed and table arms do vary with the tie seed: table hits span up to 4 (L2b) and 9 (L4b, split 0: 174 to 183) hits across the 8 seeds, fixed up to 6 and 9. That is why the split gap is a mean over 8 seeds, and the greedy arm's own 0 spread says only that its scores have no exact ties. (2) The sds over splits (0.38, 0.62) and "0 of 8 splits above +1.0" understate the uncertainty, for the reason given in the L9b critic note: the 8 splits are group shuffles of one pool keeping 75% of the same buckets, all on one p0 fit. The entry and the addendum table state them without that caveat (table.md carries it). As an unregistered diagnostic (computed in the shell, nothing written), I drew 60 random half-pools per pool (RandomState(900 + d) row halves, then MIN 3 buckets, default_rng(d) shuffle, first quarter dropped, p0 fit of 3,000, table and fixed averaged over tie seeds 0 to 3, greedy at seed 0, 10% budget, budgets 105 to 119 on L2b and 92 to 111 on L4b). greedy_strata minus the table has mean -2.62, sd 1.61, range -5.80 to +1.85 on L2b (1 of 60 above +1.0) and mean -2.15, sd 1.85, range -5.79 to +1.64 on L4b (2 of 60 above +1.0), so the resampling sd is about 3 to 4 times the split sd, and the chance that both pools clear +1.0 in the same replicate is about 0.06% if the pools are treated as independent. This is firmer than the L9b kill on L4b (greedy_prior minus table on the same draws: -2.02, sd 2.69, 10 of 60 above +1.0). The regime differs from the registered one (half the rows), so these figures are indicative only. (3) "The L9b-to-L18a moves are inside the pool-resample noise" cites the sd of the unpaired gap (near 2.5 pp) as the yardstick for a paired change between two arms on the same rows. The conclusion holds, but on the right yardstick: on the 60 half-pool draws greedy_strata minus greedy_prior has mean -0.18, sd 1.62 on L2b (positive in 23 draws, negative in 27, zero in 10) and mean -0.12, sd 1.98 on L4b (26, 25, 9). Per registered split the change is 0 to +2 hits on L2b (+1, +2, +2, 0, +2, 0, +2, 0; mean +0.49 pp, sd 0.43) and -5 to +2 hits on L4b (-1, +2, -2, -1, -5, +1, -5, -2; mean -0.80 pp, sd 1.25), one hit being about 0.45 pp. The L2b changes are never negative, but they are 0 to 2 picks out of 219 to 236 on shared rows and are not a measured effect. "No direction is claimed" is the right reading. (4) "Claim 2 stays not_supported on this pair for learned arms at 3,000 p0 labels" is wider than what was tested and than the entry's own scope sentence ("this estimator only"). Two logistic arms were tested (six features, L9b; six features plus cell indicators, L18a). The supported statement is "for the logistic scorer family, with and without cell indicators". Scorers that rank by the cell posterior are untested, as follow-up 1 says. (5) Follow-ups, cautions only, none run. L19a: its refits add at most 236 verified labels to a 3,000-label posterior with k0 50, which is the argument the entry uses to downgrade L18b, so its picks should sit close to the frozen table's and the +1.0 pass bar is likely out of reach; online stratum-posterior updating over a table was also already run at 200 labels in L10a and L11a, so only the label count is new. Its information value is closer to medium than medium-high. L19c: the +8.58 pp for the remaining rows is the L10a critic's stand-in at 200 labels, not a run output; the 48 L16a cuts (p5 +1.89, p95 +8.25, registered test rows +1.22) are 200-label tables, so L19c must say it compares the 200-label table, not the 3,000-label one (+3.39 on the test rows in L6a); and in the backlog block the hypothesis (a fresh rollout reproduces the low +1.22) is the opposite direction to the pass rule (inside p5 to p95 means not reproducing it), so read a kill there as "question source" and a pass as "rollout run". (6) Cosmetic: the script docstring says "Everything else is as in L18a" where L9b is meant; the script was not edited after the run. Corrected verdict: KILL on both pools, unchanged, on the registered rule alone (L2b -1.89, L4b -2.39 against +1.0), with all 288 spot-replayed hit counts equal to the saved ones. Read as a clear kill on both pools once pool-sampling noise is counted (1 and 2 of 60 half-pool draws above +1.0). Claim 2 stays not_supported on this pair for the logistic scorer family at 3,000 p0 labels; the missing-interaction reading is rejected for that estimator only; the cell-posterior scorers remain untested; claim 3 unchanged. The frozen table stays the deliverable.

## L19c 2026-10-19 registered (rule fixed before the run, no result yet)

Hypothesis: the registered-row shortfall is a property of these questions, not of the L4b rollout run. The 200-label frozen-minus-fixed gain on the 1,319 registered GSM8K test rows was +1.22 pp (L6a, pids 0 to 1318), against about +8.6 pp on the remaining L4b rows (+8.58 pp, the L10a critic's 200-label stand-in, not a run output). A fresh rollout of the same 2,792-prompt pool with a new sampling seed should reproduce a test-row gain at or below the 5th percentile of the 48 L16a train cuts. The backlog's null sentence contradicts this hypothesis, so the pass and kill rules below, as written in the backlog, decide the verdict.

Reading fixed in advance: a KILL means the low registered-row gain reproduced on fresh rollouts, which points to the questions (the hypothesis's direction). A PASS means the gain fell inside the L16a band, so the registered-row shortfall did not reproduce, which points to the L4b rollout run. Above p95 is inconclusive.

Registered rule: the metric is the frozen minus plain fixed hit rate at a 10% budget, in pp, on pids 0 to 1318 of runs/L19c/feats.parquet, for 200-label tables. Subset i (i = 0 to 19) is 200 distinct p0 prompts drawn with numpy RandomState(100 + i).choice(3000, 200, replace=False). Each table is fitted once on its 200 prompts with k0 50, prior 1 minus agreement, strata agreement x n_distinct x any_truncated, and frozen. A subset mean is the mean over split seeds 0 to 7 of the gap, each split averaged over 8 tie-break seeds. The verdict value is the mean of the 20 subset means (sd ddof 1 over subset means). PASS if the value is above +1.0 pp and inside [p5, p95] of the 48 L16a L2b cut means (p5 +1.89 pp, p95 +8.25 pp, read from runs/L16a/result.json at run time). KILL if the value is at or below +1.0 pp, or below p5. Inconclusive if above p95 (an unregistered outcome, neither pass nor kill).

Design: one GPU job through bin/gpu_run.sh, the unchanged L4b pool script with --G 8 --seed 2 (L4b used seed 1) and then the unchanged l2b_feats.py. The pool is L4b's exactly: pids 0 to 1318 are the GSM8K test rows, pids 1319 to 2791 the remaining train rows, disjointness from the L1b and L2b pools asserted in the script. The question embeddings and the cell100 partition are therefore the same as L4b; only the rollout columns change. Expected about 17 min, hard cap 45 min. Analysis is CPU only in the shell: avrl/exp/l19c_test_replicate.py, a copy of avrl/exp/l6a_test_rows.py (L6a unchanged), with four changes: (1) the L5a stand-in reproduction gate is replaced by a self-check (below); (2) the verdict uses the +1.0 pp and L16a band rule above instead of the +2.0 pp rule; (3) the 200-label gain on pids 1319 and up is added as context; (4) raw hit counts are written to result.json. Budget int(0.10 x n_sim), k0 50, 20 subsets, split seeds 0 to 7, 8 tie-break seeds.

Gates before any verdict (a failed gate writes verdict GATE_FAILED and exits 1): (a) self-check: plain fixed at 10% and the 3,000-label gain on pids 0 to 1318 are recomputed in a second pass and must match the first within 0.05 pp, with fixed in (0, 100). The L4b values (84.50%, +3.39 pp) do not apply to fresh rollouts and are not compared. (b) spend: every policy takes exactly int(0.10 x n_sim) distinct docs on every split, seed and table. (c) every subset has 200 distinct prompts, fixed picks are identical across all tables, and no test question text occurs in p0.

Context only (outside the verdict): the 3,000-label gain on pids 0 to 1318 (mean over split seeds 0 to 7), and the 200-label gain on pids 1319 and up, using the same 20 frozen tables and the same seeds. The critic check (avrl/exp/l19c_check.py, written to runs/L19c/critic) reimplements the subset tables, splits and tie-broken picks without importing the L1B or L6a code, and compares all 160 subset-split hit counts with result.json.

Files: avrl/exp/L19c.sh (launch and analyze runbook), avrl/exp/l19c_test_replicate.py, avrl/exp/l19c_check.py, avrl/runs/L19c/ (rollouts, groups, feats, analysis/, critic/, result.json, table.md). No ssh, no pip, no edits to any p0 or earlier-run script.

## L19c 2026-10-19 pass (fresh rollouts: registered test-row 200-label gain +2.17 pp, inside the L16a band; the L6a shortfall did not reproduce)

Result against the registered rule. The mean of the 20 subset means on pids 0 to 1318 is +2.17 pp (sd over subset means 1.39; 16 of 20 subsets above +1.0). The L16a band is [+1.89, +8.25] pp (5th and 95th percentiles of the 48 cut means, numpy linear, from runs/L16a/result.json). The value is above +1.0 and inside the band, so the verdict is PASS.

Hypothesis check. The hypothesis predicted a fresh-rollout gain at or below p5 (+1.89 pp). The observed value is 0.28 pp above p5, so the prediction failed. Under the reading fixed before the run, the registered-row shortfall did not reproduce on fresh rollouts of the same questions, which points to the L4b rollout run and not to the question set.

Numbers (all from runs/L19c/analysis/result.json unless noted):
- Plain fixed at 10% on the test rows 86.40% (L6a 84.50%); frozen 200-label tables 88.57% (L6a 85.73%).
- Context, 3,000-label table on the same rows: +3.46 pp, sd 2.24, 7 of 8 split gaps above +1.0 (L6a +3.39 pp).
- Context, 200-label tables on pids 1319 and up (1,473 rows, 17.8% proxy error): +7.10 pp, sd 3.84, 20 of 20 subsets above +1.0.
- Rollouts: 2,792 prompts, G 8, 22,336 rollouts, 968 s. Rollout accuracy 0.5684 (L4b 0.5821); group proxy error 0.2206 (L4b 0.2138); test-row proxy error 26.8% (L6a 26.2%).

Gates, all passed. Self-check: the second pass reproduces plain fixed and the 3,000-label gap exactly (max abs diff 0.000000 pp). Spend: 5,376 checks, 0 failures. Every subset has 200 distinct prompts, fixed picks are identical across all tables, and 0 test question texts occur in p0.

Control (same script, old L4b features, outside the verdict; runs/L19c/control_L4b/result.json): 200-label +1.2223 pp and 3,000-label +3.3924 pp, both equal to runs/L6a/result.json, and all 160 per-split subset gaps equal the L6a subset_detail (max abs diff 0.000000 pp). The 200-label gain on pids 1319 and up on L4b rollouts is +8.58 pp, matching the L10a stand-in. So the copied script reproduces L6a exactly, and the difference between L6a and L19c comes from the rollouts.

Reading the margin. The value is 0.28 pp above the band floor. With 20 subset means at sd 1.39, the standard error of the mean is about 0.31 pp, so the pass is marginal. The registered-row 200-label gain is +1.22 pp on one rollout draw and +2.17 pp on the other, and the second draw also clears the +2.0 pp bar that L6a used. The contrast between test rows and remaining rows moved from +7.36 pp on L4b rollouts (8.58 minus 1.22) to +4.93 pp on L19c (7.10 minus 2.17). Two rollout draws are one replicate: they do not give an effect size for the rollout run.

Claim status: not changed by this entry. The pass removes the registered-row shortfall as evidence for a question-source effect. It does not establish a registered-row gain by itself, and the 3,000-label table remains the deliverable. Follow-ups, not registered: a second fresh rollout seed on the same pool would show how much of the +1.22 to +2.17 spread is draw-to-draw noise.

Critic note (L19c, 2026-10-19). avrl/exp/l19c_check.py recomputed the 20 subset tables, the cell100 splits for split seeds 0 to 7, the fixed and frozen picks for tie seeds 0 to 7, and the 3,000-label table, from the p0 and L19c feature files, without importing l1b_tuned_threshold or any analysis code. All 2,560 subset-split hit counts and the 3,000-label hit counts equal runs/L19c/analysis/result.json, the gaps agree to 0.00e+00 pp, the subset means, sd, p5 and p95 agree, the verdict is PASS on both, and the check reports 0 problems (runs/L19c/critic/check.json). The L19c questions and cell100 partition equal runs/L4b in pid order. The rollout log has one vLLM "EngineCore died unexpectedly" line at engine teardown, written in the same second as the saved rollouts and summary (the same line appears in runs/L4b/run.log); exit_code is 0 and every output file is complete.

## L19c 2026-10-19 pass

Hypothesis (registered at line 1137): the registered-row shortfall of the 200-label frozen table (+1.22 pp on the 1,319 GSM8K test rows in L6a, against +8.58 pp on the remaining rows) is a property of the questions. Fresh rollouts of the same 2,792-prompt pool with a new sampling seed should give a test-row gain at or below the 5th percentile of the 48 L16a cut means (+1.89 pp). Registered rule, as written in the backlog: PASS if the value is above +1.0 pp and inside [p5, p95]; KILL if at or below +1.0 pp or below p5; inconclusive above p95. The hypothesis predicts a KILL, and the verdict below follows the rule as written.

What was run: one gpu_run.sh job, exit 0, 968 s of generation (about 20 min wall by file times). avrl/exp/l4b_rollouts_pool3.py with --G 8 --seed 2 (L4b used seed 1) on the L4b pool: Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct, GSM8K, 2,792 prompts (pids 0 to 1318 are the registered test rows, pids 1319 to 2791 the remaining train rows), 22,336 rollouts. Then avrl/exp/l2b_feats.py. Fit: the unchanged p0 pool (3,000 prompts, runs/p0/feats.parquet), 36 strata (agreement x n_distinct x any_truncated), k0 50, prior 1 minus agreement, each table fitted once and frozen. 200-label tables: 20 subsets of 200 p0 prompts (numpy RandomState(100 + i)). Eval: pids 0 to 1318 of runs/L19c/feats.parquet. Budget 10% (int(0.10 x n_sim) distinct docs per split). Split seeds 0 to 7, 8 tie-break seeds each. Gap is frozen minus plain fixed, paired by split and tie seed. Analysis: avrl/exp/l19c_test_replicate.py (copy of l6a_test_rows.py), CPU, main venv with -I.

Verification (this step, from the files, not the run report):
- exit_code 0, analysis_exit_code 0, done.flag and analysis_done.flag present. critic/check.json has problems [] and critic value +2.1723 pp, equal to the result.
- Verdict value recomputed from the 20 per-split gap lists in analysis/result.json: +2.1723 pp, sd 1.3946 (ddof 1), 16 of 20 subsets above +1.0, min -1.43, max +3.71. Matches the run report and table.md.

Verdict: PASS under the registered rule. The value (+2.17 pp) is above +1.0 and inside the band [+1.89, +8.25]. The hypothesis predicted a KILL (a value at or below p5) and did not get it: the value is 0.28 pp above p5. The margin is small. The L16a critic estimated the spread of p5 under resampling at about 0.36 pp, so the pass sits inside the uncertainty of the band floor. Read it as a marginal pass.

What it changes about the thesis claims:
- The 3,000-label table, the deliverable, is stable across eval-rollout draws on the registered rows: +3.39 pp (L6a) and +3.46 pp (L19c). Its fit pool (p0) has one rollout draw, so fit-side variance is still untested (follow-up L20b).
- The 200-label table on the registered rows is positive on both draws (+1.22, +2.17) but straddles the band floor. The L6a kill was weaker than it read. The gap to the 3,000-label table is 2.17 pp on the first draw and 1.29 pp on the second.
- The L16a kill was partly a property of the L4b rollout run: with fresh rollouts the same questions give +2.17 pp, inside the band. The row-set contrast did not go away: the test rows still trail the remaining rows by +4.93 pp (against +7.36 pp on L4b rollouts). The run explains part of the shortfall and the row set may explain the rest, but the split is not resolved (follow-up L20a).
- The pass section above (line 1153, written by the run) says the pass "removes the registered-row shortfall as evidence for a question-source effect". That overreaches. Two draws cannot remove a row-set contrast that persists on both runs.
- Claim 2 (a learned allocator admits fewer corrupted rewards than any fixed rule at equal budget): not_supported, scope unchanged. The narrow registered-row statement (the frozen table beats plain fixed at 10%) now holds on two rollout draws at 3,000 labels and on both draws at 200 labels. "Any fixed rule" still has one rule tested (L17c has not run), the table is fitted on labels rather than learned online, and the equal-label comparison is untested (L6a critic note 3).
- Claim 3 (the edge comes from structure in prompt space): not_supported, unchanged. All 36 strata are rollout-side, so this run gives no prompt-space evidence.

Follow-ups (L20a to L20c in lit/avrl/EXPERIMENT_BACKLOG.md under "Proposed by the loop, iteration 20 (after L19c)", none run):
1. L20a, stratum-mix and headroom decomposition of the row-set contrast (CPU, existing feats). Reweight the remaining rows to the test rows' 36-stratum mix and recompute the 200-label contrast. Pass (mix explains it): reweighted contrast at or below +1.0 pp on runs/L19c. Kill (it survives): above +3.0 pp. Expected information value high: it says whether the registered-row number is a sampling fact or a question-level effect, which sets how the held-out claim is worded.
2. L20b, fresh fit-pool rollouts (GPU, one gpu_run.sh job, about 20 min). Redraw the p0 rollouts with a new sampling seed, refit the 3,000-label table and the 20 subset tables, and score on the L19c test rows. Pass: 3,000-label registered-row gain above +2.0 pp. Kill: at or below +2.0 pp. Expected information value high: the fit pool has never been redrawn, and the deliverable rests on it.
3. L20c, second and third fixed rules on the fresh registered rows (CPU, existing feats). This is the registered-row half of L17c, moved to runs/L19c. Pass: the 200-label frozen table beats each added rule by more than +1.0 pp on pids 0 to 1318. Kill: either added rule at or below 0. Expected information value high for claim 2's scope: a rule that matches the table would reduce claim 2 to a statement about plain fixed.

Critic note (L19c, adversarial re-check, 2026-10-19). Checked the registration (line 1137 and the backlog L19c block), the pass sections, runs/L19c/result.json (including raw hit counts), analysis/result.json, table.md, run.log, analysis/run.log, summary.json, exit codes and flags, rollouts.parquet, groups.parquet, feats.parquet, control_L4b/result.json, critic/check.json, runs/L16a/result.json, runs/L6a/result.json, runs/L4b (rollouts, feats, summary, run.log), exp/L19c.sh, exp/l19c_test_replicate.py, exp/l19c_check.py, exp/l4b_rollouts_pool3.py, exp/l2b_feats.py, p0_rollouts.py and the installed vLLM source (vllm/v1/engine/parallel_sampling.py). What checks out. Every figure in the entry matches the raw files: value +2.1723 pp, sd 1.3946 (ddof 1), 16 of 20 subset means above +1.0 (min -1.43, max +3.71), p5 +1.8879 and p95 +8.2460 from the 48 values_pp of runs/L16a (median +4.01), fixed 86.40% and frozen 88.57% (L6a 84.50% and 85.73%), the 3,000-label gap +3.46 pp (sd 2.24, 7 of 8 split gaps above +1.0), the remaining-row gain +7.10 pp (sd 3.84, 20 of 20 subsets above +1.0, 1,473 rows, 17.8% error), test-row error 26.84%, all-prompt error 22.06% (L4b 21.38%), rollout accuracy 0.5684 (L4b 0.5821) and 51.95% on the test rows (L4b 53.82%), 22,336 rollouts in 968 s, the L4b control (+1.2223 and +3.3924, all 160 gaps equal to L6a with max abs diff 0, remaining rows +8.58), the 5,376 spend checks (42 pools x 2 policies x 8 splits x 8 tie seeds, 0 failures), n_sim 957 to 1,017 with budget = int(0.10 x n_sim) on every split, the contrasts +4.93 and +7.36, and the 2.17 and 1.29 gaps to the 3,000-label table. I recomputed the 20 subset means from the 160 saved split gaps and the gaps from the raw hit counts (max diff 6e-15 pp). Rule check: +2.1723 is above +1.0 and inside [+1.8879, +8.2460], so PASS follows from the registered thresholds; the 0.28 pp margin over p5 is right. No leakage: agreement, n_distinct, any_truncated, mean_tokens and log_qlen recompute exactly from rollouts.parquet answers and token counts (gold-free), the frozen score is the table posterior with prior 1 minus agreement, gold enters only the 200 or 3,000 p0 fit labels and the hit count, 0 test questions occur in p0, no NaN in the features. Budget accounting is equal (same int(0.10 x n_sim) distinct docs per policy, split and tie seed; asserted in 5,376 checks and in my own check). Logs: exit codes 0, one "EngineCore died unexpectedly" line after "Shutdown complete" and after the outputs were written (also present once in runs/L4b/run.log), no truncation beyond the 512-token cap that the any_truncated feature already carries (2.76% of rollouts, L4b 3.09%). The independent check l19c_check.py is a real recomputation (own code, no import of the analysis modules). Corrections follow. (1) The rollouts are not an independent draw, and the freshness check in the entry was misaligned. The rollout script passes seed = a.seed in SamplingParams with n = 8, and vLLM 0.19.1 gives child sample k the seed seed + k (parallel_sampling.py line 80). L4b (seed 1) therefore sampled with per-sample seeds 1 to 8 and L19c (seed 2) with 2 to 9: 7 of 8 sample streams per prompt are shared, shifted by one slot (L19c slot k has the seed of L4b slot k+1). The entry compared the same (pid, k), where only 0.8% of token counts agree, which is the wrong alignment. On the aligned pairs (19,544) 44.3% have the same token count and 43.0% the same token count and extracted answer, against 0.19% on an unaligned shift; 71.1% have the same answer against 43.8% unaligned; the within-prompt correlation of token counts is 0.83 aligned against about -0.02 to -0.2 unaligned (demeaned). The runs are not identical because exact repeats break under GPU nondeterminism, and the group features move: the 36-stratum cell is the same for only 32.6% of prompts (29.3% on test rows), but agreement correlates 0.87 between the runs and proxy_error is identical on 90.7% of prompts (89.4% on test rows); I have no independent-draw baseline for those group-level figures. So L19c is a seed-shifted, partly overlapping second run, not a fresh replicate, and "fresh rollouts", "two rollout draws" and "stable across eval-rollout draws" (3.39 and 3.46) should read "a partly overlapping second draw". (2) The attribution drawn from the pair is not supported, and the stated uncertainty is the wrong one. The 0.31 pp standard error (sd 1.39 over 20 subset means) covers only variation across fit subsets, all scored on one eval draw of one set of rows. The eval side is larger: runs/L16a's row-level bootstrap on the same registered rows has sd 1.57 pp (5th to 95th percentile -1.51 to +3.75), the 8 per-split means of the 20 subsets have sd 1.18 on L19c, the L6a-to-L19c move is +0.95 pp with a per-subset paired sd of 2.32 and a subset-level correlation between the two runs of 0.03, and L16a put the sd of p5 itself at 0.36 pp. The 0.28 pp margin over p5, the 1.17 pp margin over +1.0 and the 0.95 pp move from L6a all sit inside that noise. The statements "points to the L4b rollout run and not the question set", "the L16a kill was partly a property of the L4b rollout run" and "the run explains part of the shortfall" are therefore not supported; this pair separates neither the rollout run, nor the question source, nor sampling noise. The test-minus-remaining contrast (+4.93, +7.36) also comes from overlapping draws. (3) Follow-up cautions, none run. Any independent redraw needs a seed at least 8 away from every earlier one (L4b 1 to 8, L19c 2 to 9, p0 0 to 7; for example --seed 100). L20b as written (p0 copy with sampling seed 2) would share 6 of 8 sample streams with p0, and p0_rollouts.py uses --seed as the prompt-draw random_state as well (line 16), so the copy must keep random_state 0 for the 3,000 prompts and take the sampling seed separately. A "second fresh rollout seed" on the L19c pool should use the same rule. Corrected verdict: PASS on the registered numeric rule, unchanged (+2.1723 pp, above +1.0 and inside [+1.8879, +8.2460]), read as a marginal pass from one eval draw that overlaps L4b in 7 of 8 sample seeds; it neither shows that the registered-row shortfall belongs to the rollout run nor that it belongs to the questions, so that attribution stays unresolved (L20a and an independent-seed redraw are the discriminating steps). Claim 2 (not_supported) and claim 3 (not_supported) are unchanged, the 3,000-label table stays the deliverable, and the pass section's sentence that the pass "removes the registered-row shortfall as evidence for a question-source effect" is withdrawn along with the "points to the L4b rollout run" reading.

## L20b 2026-10-19 registered (rule fixed before the run, no result yet)

Hypothesis: the 3,000-label table's registered-row gain over plain fixed at 10% (+3.39 pp on runs/L6a, +3.46 pp on runs/L19c) holds when the fit pool and the test rows are both redrawn with sampling seeds that share no sample stream with any earlier pool. The L19c critic found that L6a and L19c share 7 of 8 sample streams per prompt, so the registered-row evidence is one effective eval draw, and the p0 fit pool has never been redrawn. Null: the gain on the fresh draws is at or below +2.0 pp.

Registered rule: the metric is the frozen 3,000-label table minus plain fixed hit rate at a 10% budget, in pp, on pids 0 to 1318 of the fresh test pool. The value is the mean over split seeds 0 to 7 of the split gap, each split gap the mean over 8 tie-break seeds. PASS if the value is above +2.0 pp. KILL if it is at or below +2.0 pp. There is no inconclusive band. Context only, outside the verdict: plain fixed and frozen hit rates on the test rows, the 20-subset 200-label gain (numpy RandomState(100 + i) draws of 200 fit prompts, each table fitted once and frozen), and proxy error on both fresh pools, compared with L6a (+3.39 pp, plain fixed 84.50%) and L19c (+3.46 pp, plain fixed 86.40%).

Design: one GPU job through bin/gpu_run.sh (45 min cap, about 25 min of generation plus two vLLM loads expected). First avrl/exp/l20b_fit_rollouts.py on the 3,000 p0 prompts: the p0 draw (gsm8k_train.sample(n=3000, random_state=0)), asserted equal to runs/p0/groups.parquet questions in order, with the vLLM and SamplingParams seed set to 100 (streams 100 to 107). Then avrl/exp/l20b_test_rollouts.py on the 1,319 GSM8K test rows, asserted equal to runs/L19c pids 0 to 1318 in order, with seed 200 (streams 200 to 207). Model, G 8, temperature 1.0, top_p 1.0, max_tokens 512, answer extraction, proxy (majority vote) and gold are unchanged. On CPU, avrl/exp/l20b_feats.py attaches cell30, cell100 and cell300 by question text from the registered partitions (runs/p0/feats.parquet for the fit pool, runs/L19c/feats.parquet for the test rows); KMeans is not re-run, so the cell100 split is the registered one and only the rollout columns change. avrl/exp/l20b_replicate.py is a copy of avrl/exp/l19c_test_replicate.py with the verdict replaced by the 3,000-label rule above, the L16a band dropped, and no remaining-train rows (this pool has none). avrl/exp/l20b_check.py is the critic: it recomputes the 3,000-label table and the 20 subset tables from the feature files with no import of analysis code, compares every hit count, the value and the verdict with result.json, and adds a stream-overlap diagnostic against p0 and L19c (context only).

Gates before any verdict (a failed gate writes GATE_FAILED and exits 1): (a) self-check: plain fixed at 10% and the 3,000-label gap are recomputed in a second pass and match within 0.05 pp, with fixed in (0, 100). (b) spend: every policy takes exactly int(0.10 x n_sim) distinct docs on every split, seed and table. (c) every subset has 200 distinct prompts, fixed picks are identical across all tables, and no test question text occurs in the fit pool.

Backlog note: the L20b block in lit/avrl/EXPERIMENT_BACKLOG.md proposed sampling seed 2 and one script named avrl/exp/l20b_p0_rollouts_seed2.py. Seed 2 shares 6 of 8 sample streams with p0 (the L19c critic note), so the run uses seeds 100 and 200 and the two scripts above instead.

Reading limit: this redraws rollouts only. The 3,000 fit questions and the 1,319 test questions stay fixed, so question-sampling variance is not measured. A KILL is a kill of the registered-row gain on fresh rollouts, not of the question set.

Files: avrl/exp/L20b.sh (launch and analyze runbook), avrl/exp/l20b_fit_rollouts.py, avrl/exp/l20b_test_rollouts.py, avrl/exp/l20b_feats.py, avrl/exp/l20b_replicate.py, avrl/exp/l20b_check.py, and avrl/runs/L20b/ (fit/, test/, analysis/, critic/, result.json, table.md, done.flag). No ssh, no pip, no edit to any p0 or earlier-run script.

## L20b 2026-10-19 pass (fresh fit and test rollouts: registered-row 3,000-label gain +5.35 pp, above +2.0; the redraw moves both sides, so it does not attribute the change)

What was run: one gpu_run.sh job on willow (28 MiB used at launch), exit 0, finished 02:46. avrl/exp/l20b_fit_rollouts.py on the 3,000 p0 prompts (draw random_state 0, asserted equal to runs/p0/groups.parquet questions in order), sampling seed 100: 24,000 rollouts in 847 s. Then avrl/exp/l20b_test_rollouts.py on the 1,319 GSM8K test rows (asserted equal to runs/L19c pids 0 to 1318 in order), sampling seed 200: 10,552 rollouts in 410 s. Model Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct, G 8, temperature 1.0, top_p 1.0, max_tokens 512, unchanged. CPU, main venv with -I: avrl/exp/l20b_feats.py (cell30, cell100, cell300 attached by question text from runs/p0/feats.parquet and runs/L19c/feats.parquet, no KMeans re-run), avrl/exp/l20b_replicate.py (copy of l19c_test_replicate.py, verdict on the 3,000-label table), avrl/exp/l20b_check.py (critic). Runbook: avrl/exp/L20b.sh.

| sd over split gaps | 1.30 | 2.24 | 2.21 |

| plain fixed at 10%, test rows | 88.01% | 86.40% | 84.50% |
| frozen at 10%, test rows | 93.35% | 89.86% | 87.90% |
| 200-label subset gain, mean of 20 (context only) | +3.74 (sd 2.01, range +0.48 to +6.30, 15 of 20 above +2.0) | +2.17 | +1.22 |
| proxy error, fit pool | 14.63% (p0 14.53%) | 14.53% | 14.53% |

Split gaps, L20b: +7.60, +5.38, +4.74, +5.56, +5.05, +5.43, +2.97, +6.06.

Gates (all PASS in result.json): self-check, plain fixed 88.0068% and gap +5.3481 pp in a second pass, max abs diff 0.000000 pp (tol 0.05); spend 2,816 checks, 0 failures; every subset has 200 distinct prompts; fixed picks identical across all tables; 0 test question texts in the fit pool. Logs: exit codes 0 for both GPU steps and all CPU steps. The "EngineCore died unexpectedly" line appears once, after "Shutdown complete" and after the outputs were written, as in L19c.

Stream check (critic, context only): the fit rollouts against p0 and the test rollouts against L19c. At the aligned sample index, 0.61% (fit vs p0) and 0.69% (test vs L19c) of (prompt, sample) pairs have identical token counts, against 0.63% and 0.61% averaged over the other seven index shifts. So the aligned pairs carry no shared sample stream. This is the independence the L19c critic asked for: the earlier L19c and L4b pair had 44% identical token counts at the aligned index.

Verdict against the pre-registered rule: the value is +5.35 pp, above +2.0: PASS. The kill condition (at or below +2.0) is not triggered. The critic (avrl/exp/l20b_check.py, output runs/L20b/critic/check.json) recomputed the 3,000-label table and all 20 subset tables from the feature files with numpy and pandas only. All 2,560 subset-split hit counts and the 3,000-label hit counts equal result.json, the gap agrees to 0.00e+00 pp, the verdict is PASS on both, and the critic reports 0 problems. Both the test-row pool and the fit pool equal their registered question lists in order, with the registered cell columns.

Reading, which the registered rule does not settle:
1. The change is not attributed. Both the fit pool and the test pool were redrawn in the same run. The gain rose from +3.4 to +5.3 pp, and the plain fixed rate on the test rows rose from 84.5% to 88.0%. A fresh eval draw moves the baseline as well as the gain. The 3,000-label result from L19c and L6a was therefore not stable across draws in size, even though it cleared the bar both times. Only the L20d decomposition below can assign the change to the fit draw or the eval draw.
2. The L19c and L20b difference is +1.9 pp. The only eval-side noise measure in the ledger is the row-level bootstrap on the 200-label tables in L16a (sd 1.57 pp), not on the 3,000-label table, so the size of the difference is not measured in its own units.
3. One fit draw and one eval draw were made. The 3,000 p0 questions and the 1,319 test questions are fixed, so question-sampling variance is not measured. The test-row claim stays "the registered-row gain is above +2.0 pp on two independent rollout draws of both pools"; it does not say the gain is about +5 pp.
4. The 200-label context (+3.74 pp, 15 of 20 subsets above +2.0) is outside the verdict. It is above the L6a and L19c points but is the same kind of 200-label number that L6a killed on its own draws, so it is not a new pass.

Claim status: claim 2 wording stands unchanged (the 3,000-label table is the deliverable). The fit-side variance question left open by L19c (follow-up L20b) is now partly answered: the gain holds on a fresh fit pool and fresh test rollouts, with a larger size than before. Attribution to the fit draw is open (L20d).

Follow-ups (backlog ids L20d, under "Proposed by the loop, iteration 21 (after L20b)"; L20a and L20c stay in the backlog under iteration 20, none run):
1. L20d, CPU only, existing feats: a 2x2 decomposition of the 3,000-label gain. (a) the frozen 3,000 table fitted on runs/L20b/fit scored on runs/L19c test rows (pids 0 to 1318); (b) the frozen 3,000 table fitted on runs/p0 scored on runs/L20b/test. Pass (fit side): (a) above +2.0 pp, so the fresh fit draw alone keeps the registered gain. Kill: (a) at or below +2.0 pp, so the gain depends on the eval draw. Information value high: it says which side moved and whether the L6a and L19c figures or the L20b figure is the one to report.
2. L20a and L20c stay as written in the backlog.
3. The L20b block in lit/avrl/EXPERIMENT_BACKLOG.md (sampling seed 2, script l20b_p0_rollouts_seed2.py) is superseded by this run (seeds 100 and 200, scripts above).
