## L20b 2026-10-19 pass

**Hypothesis.** The 3,000-label frozen table's registered-row gain over plain fixed at 10% (+3.39 pp on L6a, +3.46 pp on L19c) holds when both the fit pool and the registered-row rollouts are redrawn with sampling seeds that share no sample stream with any earlier pool. Null: the gain on the fresh draws is at or below +2.0 pp.

**What was run.** Model Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct (vLLM 0.19.1, bf16, max_model_len 2048, gpu_memory_utilization 0.85). Task GSM8K. G 8, temperature 1.0, top_p 1.0, max_tokens 512, answer extraction unchanged. One gpu_run.sh job on willow (28 MiB used at launch), exit code 0.
- Fit pool: the 3,000 p0 prompts (gsm8k_train sample with random_state 0, asserted equal to runs/p0 questions in order), sampling seed 100 (streams 100 to 107), 24,000 rollouts.
- Registered rows: the 1,319 GSM8K test rows (pids 0 to 1318, asserted equal to runs/L19c in order), sampling seed 200 (streams 200 to 207), 10,552 rollouts.
- Scoring: budget 10% of n_sim (n_sim 957 to 1,017 per split, budget 95 to 101 docs), k0 50, split seeds 0 to 7, 8 tie seeds each. The 3,000-label table is fitted once on the fit pool (34 strata observed) and frozen. Context only: 20 subsets of 200 fit prompts, RandomState(100 + i).
- Scripts: avrl/exp/l20b_fit_rollouts.py, avrl/exp/l20b_test_rollouts.py, avrl/exp/l20b_feats.py, avrl/exp/l20b_replicate.py, avrl/exp/l20b_check.py. No p0 or earlier-run script edited.

**Results.** Hit rate is hits divided by budget, on the registered rows (pids 0 to 1318).

| quantity | L20b (fresh fit, fresh rows) | L19c (p0 fit, partly overlapping second eval draw, seed 2) | L6a (p0 fit, L4b eval draw, seed 1) |
|---|---|---|---|
| 3,000-label gain over plain fixed at 10%, pp | **+5.35** (split sd 1.30, 8 of 8 splits above +2.0) | +3.46 | +3.39 |
| plain fixed at 10% | 88.01% | 86.40% | 84.50% |
| frozen 3,000-label table at 10% | 93.35% | 89.86% (derived: 86.40 + 3.46) | 87.89% (derived: 84.50 + 3.39) |
| 200-label gain, mean of 20 subsets (context) | +3.74 (sd 2.01, range +0.48 to +6.30, 15 of 20 above +2.0) | +2.17 | +1.22 |
| fit proxy error | 14.63% | 14.53% (p0) | 14.53% (p0) |
| registered-row proxy error | 27.60% | 26.84% | not recorded here |

Split gaps for L20b: +7.60, +5.38, +4.74, +5.56, +5.05, +5.43, +2.97, +6.06.

**Verdict: PASS.** The value +5.35 pp is above the pre-registered pass line of +2.0 pp. The kill condition (at or below +2.0 pp) is not reached on any split (lowest split gap +2.97). Run-log gates all PASS: self-check, spend gate (2,816 checks, 0 failures), distinct subset prompts, identical fixed picks, 0 test questions in the fit pool.

**Verification note (orchestrator, 2026-10-19).** Checked against the raw files, not only the run report.
- Recomputed the 3,000-label gap from the raw hit counts in result.json (raw_3000_test): gap +5.3481 pp, split sd 1.2964 (ddof 1), fixed 88.0068%, frozen 93.3549%, largest difference from the reported split gaps 1.8e-14 pp.
- Row counts match the design: fit 3,000 groups and 24,000 rollouts; test 1,319 groups and 10,552 rollouts; 8 samples per prompt. The feature logs report all questions matched (3,000 and 1,319).
- Exit codes 0 for the GPU chain and the CPU analysis. critic/check.json: 0 problems, 2,560 subset-split gaps equal to result.json (max difference 0.00e+00 pp).
- Log scan: no traceback. Two "EngineCore died unexpectedly" lines (run.log lines 61 and 122) follow completed vLLM jobs; the chain still exited 0 and both pools have the expected row counts.
- Stream independence holds by seed arithmetic: p0 seed 0 (streams 0 to 7), L4b seed 1 (1 to 8), L19c seed 2 (2 to 9), L20b seeds 100 (100 to 107) and 200 (200 to 207). The critic's overlap check compares shifts 0 to 7 only, so it does not test an offset of 100 or 200; the no-overlap statement rests on the seed arithmetic.
- GPU after the run: willow, 28 MiB of 16,376 MiB used.

**What it changes about the thesis claims.**
- **Claim 2** (a learned allocator admits fewer corrupted rewards than any fixed rule at equal budget): not_supported, scope unchanged. The narrow statement, that the 3,000-label frozen table beats plain fixed at 10% on the registered rows, now holds on a fresh fit pool and fresh rollouts, the first redraw of the fit pool. Three limits keep the scope open:
  1. "Any fixed rule" still has one rule tested (L17c and L20c not run).
  2. The size of the gain moved from +3.4 to +5.3 pp, and the plain fixed rate moved from 84.5% to 88.0%. Report it as a gap tied to its draws, not as a rate.
  3. The 8-split sd (1.30) covers split variation on one pair of row sets, not row-sampling noise. The nearest ledger measure is the L16a row-level bootstrap on 200-label tables (sd 1.57 pp). If the 3,000-label table is similarly noisy, the +3.35 pp margin over the bar is about two such sd. That is an estimate, not a measurement (L22a measures it).
- **Config B.** The tuned, label-fed adaptive stratum rule reached +4.6 to +5.0 pp over plain fixed at 10% on earlier pools (RESEARCH_LOG lines 277 and 337). The table's +5.35 pp on fresh rows is the same size, so the table's margin over config B has not been measured on any pool. Config B is adaptive, not a fixed rule, so it does not refute claim 2 as worded, but it is the comparison that decides whether the table adds anything the adaptive rule does not (L22c).
- **Claim 3** (the edge comes from structure in prompt space): not_supported, unchanged. The table key is rollout-side (agreement, n_distinct, any_truncated). cell30, cell100 and cell300 are carried in the feature files, and the cell100 grouping forms the simulator's split, but none is in the table key, so this run gives no prompt-space evidence.

**Follow-ups** (backlog ids L22a to L22c under "Proposed by the loop, iteration 22 (after L20b verification)"; L20a, L20c and L20d stay as written, none run):
1. **L22a**, question-level bootstrap of the registered-row gain (CPU, existing feats): resample the 1,319 registered questions with replacement, 500 draws, table fixed. Pass: 2.5th percentile above +2.0 pp. Kill: at or below +2.0 pp. Expected information value high: it is the only uncertainty term the registered number lacks.
2. **L22b**, eval-only redraw (GPU, one gpu_run.sh job, about 10 min): keep the L20b fit pool and table, redraw only the 1,319 test rows with sampling seed 300 (streams 300 to 307). Pass: gap above +2.0 pp. Kill: at or below +2.0 pp. Expected information value high: with L20d cell (b) it fills a 2x2 over fit draw and eval draw on fresh rollouts, and says which side moved the L6a to L20b change.
3. **L22c**, the table against config B on the fresh registered rows (CPU, existing feats, gated): config B at floor 40 as run in avrl/exp/l3a_floor_sensitivity.py, minus the 3,000-label table on runs/L20b/test pids 0 to 1318. Gate: config B must reproduce its recorded floor-40 value on an earlier pool before the L20b rows are scored. Pass: table beats config B by more than +1.0 pp. Kill: at or below 0. Between: inconclusive. Expected information value high: it decides whether the deliverable adds anything over the adaptive rule already tested.

Files: avrl/runs/L20b/ (fit/, test/, analysis/, critic/, result.json, table.md, run.log, feats.log, exit_code, done.flag, analysis_exit_code, analysis_done.flag, cmd.sh, wrap.sh, pgid). Scripts: avrl/exp/l20b_*.py and avrl/exp/L20b.sh. This verification adds no GPU job and no run or script files.

Critic note (L20b, adversarial re-check, 2026-10-19). Checked the registration block, both pass sections, runs/L20b/result.json (including raw_3000_test hit counts), analysis/result.json, table.md, run.log, analysis/run.log, feats.log, critic/check.json and critic/run.log, exit codes and flags, cmd.sh, wrap.sh, fit and test summary.json, rollouts.parquet, groups.parquet and feats.parquet, exp/L20b.sh, l20b_fit_rollouts.py, l20b_test_rollouts.py, l20b_feats.py, l20b_replicate.py, l20b_check.py, runs/L6a and runs/L19c result.json, runs/p0 and runs/L19c groups and feats, the sampling seeds of every earlier rollout launch, and the L20b, L20d and L22 blocks of lit/avrl/EXPERIMENT_BACKLOG.md. What checks out. I recomputed the verdict with my own code (no import of l1b_tuned_threshold or any analysis module): gap +5.348118 pp, sd 1.296397 (ddof 1), plain fixed 88.0068%, frozen 93.3549%, 34 strata, split gaps +7.60, +5.38, +4.74, +5.56, +5.05, +5.43, +2.97, +6.06 equal to result.json to 4.4e-15 pp, n_sim and budget per split 973/97, 1004/100, 957/95, 990/99, 991/99, 999/99, 1015/101, 1017/101 (budget = int(0.10 x n_sim)). The 20 subset means (15 of 20 above +2.0, min +0.48, max +6.30, mean +3.74, sd 2.01) and the table.md values match. No leakage: agreement, n_distinct, any_truncated and mean_tokens recompute exactly from rollouts.parquet answers and token counts in both pools, majority and proxy_error recompute from the answers against gold with 0 mismatches, no NaN in the table key or cell100; gold enters only the 3,000 fit labels and the hit count. The fit questions equal runs/p0 in order with equal gold, the test questions equal runs/L19c pids 0 to 1318 in order with equal gold, 0 question texts are shared between the pools, and cell100 in both pools equals the registered column. Budget accounting is equal: both policies take exactly the budget in distinct docs (asserted in my code and in the 2,816 recorded checks, which is 22 pools x 8 splits x 8 tie seeds x 2 policies). Seeds: every earlier rollout launch used sampling seed 0, 1 or 2, so streams 100 to 107 and 200 to 207 overlap none; the identical-token shares (0.61% fit vs p0, 0.69% test vs L19c at the aligned index; 0.63% and 0.61% at the other shifts) are at chance. Logs: both exit codes 0, no traceback, 24,000 and 10,552 rollouts at 8 per prompt, 847 s and 410 s; truncation at the 512-token cap is 1.70% of fit rollouts and 2.15% of test rollouts (any_truncated on 9.77% and 12.13% of prompts), already carried by the any_truncated key; empty extracted answers 0.025% and 0.047%. The independent check l20b_check.py imports only numpy and pandas. Rule check: the registered rule (PASS above +2.0 pp, KILL at or below, no inconclusive band) matches the task statement and the backlog; +5.3481 is above +2.0 and the lowest split gap is +2.97, so PASS follows. Corrections follow. (1) In the first pass section's table, the row "split gaps above +2.0" lists L19c as 7 of 8. The raw L19c split gaps (+6.57, +6.12, +1.32, +4.67, +1.77, +2.02, +0.87, +4.33) give 5 of 8 above +2.0; 7 of 8 is the above +1.0 count from the L19c section. L6a 6 of 8 (7 of 8 above +1.0) and L20b 8 of 8 are right. (2) The first pass section says the "EngineCore died unexpectedly" line appears once; run.log has it twice (lines 61 and 122), once after each vLLM job, after Shutdown complete and after the outputs were written, as the later verification section says. (3) Minor: the L6a frozen rate is 87.90% (87.8956 from the unrounded 84.5032 + 3.3924), not the 87.89% in the later section, and the GPU chain's exit_code and done.flag were written at 02:45 (test outputs 02:45, analysis 02:46), not "finished 02:46". (4) The log now has two sections headed "L20b 2026-10-19 pass" (the run's, which holds corrections 1 and 2, and the later verification); remove the run's when the log is next tidied, as with L19c. (5) The stated margin is looser than it reads. "About two such sd" borrows the 1.57 pp sd of the L16a bootstrap, which was run on the 200-label tables. A rough question-level bootstrap I ran on the 3,000-label table itself (300 resamples with replacement of the 1,319 registered rows, table fixed, split seeds 0 to 7, 4 tie seeds, own code) gives sd 2.04 pp, 2.5th percentile +0.33, 5th percentile +1.14, median +4.37, with 12.3% of resamples at or below +2.0. Its mean (+4.29) sits below the point value because resampled duplicates enter the simulation as separate docs, so it is biased down and probably wider than a clean estimate; it is not a substitute for L22a. Read the +3.35 pp margin over the bar as about 1.6 such sd, and do not assume the L22a bar (2.5th percentile above +2.0) will be met. (6) Scope not restated in the entry: the 3,000 fit labels are free to the frozen policy and zero to plain fixed. Test-time spend is equal, but the equal-label comparison stays untested (L6a critic note 3), so "beats plain fixed at equal budget" means equal verification budget, not equal label cost. Corrected verdict: PASS on the registered rule, unchanged (+5.3481 pp, above +2.0, 8 of 8 split gaps above +2.0, lowest +2.97). It is a point-estimate pass on one fresh fit draw and one fresh eval draw with independent sample streams. It shows that the registered-row gain survives an independent rollout redraw of both pools; it does not establish a size of about +5 pp, does not attribute the move from +3.4 (L20d and L22b), and the margin over the bar is about 1.6 bootstrap sd. Claim 2 and claim 3 (both not_supported) are unchanged, and the 3,000-label table stays the deliverable.
